# Model Interpretations

Model renditions are study examples, not the only acceptable wording.

## D001 — Applying for an ABN

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a small-business adviser. I’m calling about a new sole-trader registration. I’ve found the enquiry. Before we get into the details, tell me what you need resolved.
**Model:** 早晨。呢度係小型企業顧問。我聯絡你係關於新成立嘅獨資經營登記。我搵到你個查詢喇。講細節之前，你最想解決嘅係咩呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 而家我最憂心係可能要延遲開業或者營運。我想喺下星期五之前處理好。
**Model:** My main worry is that I could have to delay starting or running the business. I’d like this resolved before next Friday.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What we need to look at is Australian Business Number, or ABN. official business details must match the entity actually trading. I’ll check the details on your case now.
**Model:** 呢個情況主要關乎澳洲商業號碼，即係 ABN。正式商業資料要同實際經營嘅實體一致。不如我哋先核對你而家嘅資料，再決定下一步。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我份文件寫住計劃九月一日開始營業。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
**Model:** My paperwork says trading is planned to start on 1 September. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The record matches that detail. Next, submit the correct application or update and keep the confirmation. Check every date and number before submitting.
**Model:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係提交正確申請或者更新資料，並保留確認紀錄。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗有相身份證明同稅務資料。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
**Model:** I have photo ID and tax details. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the business online portal. Upload photo ID and tax details, then save the receipt or reference.
**Model:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用商業網上平台。上載有相身份證明同稅務資料，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能要延遲開業或者營運。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
**Model:** How long should I wait? I’m anxious that I may have to delay starting or running the business. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about five business days. Meanwhile, keep using only verified business details until any change is confirmed. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
**Model:** 預大約五個工作日。期間，任何更改確認之前只使用已核實嘅商業資料。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果最後個結果我都唔同意，我應該先攞書面理由，定係即刻申請覆核？
**Model:** What if I don’t agree with the outcome, should I ask for written reasons first, or request a review straight away?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask the agency to check or correct the record in writing.
**Model:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，要求相關部門以書面核對或者更正紀錄。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。
**Model:** Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Fine, I’ll do it that way and keep proof of the date I sent it.
**Model:** 好，我會咁樣做，亦會留返提交日期嘅證明。

## D002 — Registering a business name

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝你打嚟小型企業顧問。我見到有一個關於擬登記嘅商業名稱嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。
**Model:** Thanks for calling a small-business adviser. I can see an enquiry about a proposed business name. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I received an update today. It mentions registered business name, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.
**Model:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到註冊商業名稱。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個詞重要，因為正式商業資料要同實際經營嘅實體一致。你目前紀錄亦顯示擬用名稱係 Harbour Home Repairs。
**Model:** That term matters because official business details must match the entity actually trading. Your current record also says the proposed name is Harbour Home Repairs.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the end of this month, because I may have to delay starting or running the business.
**Model:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺今個月底之前知道，因為可能要延遲開業或者營運。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要提交正確申請或者更新資料，並保留確認紀錄。
**Model:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, submit the correct application or update and keep the confirmation.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve gathered ABN details and contact information. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗ABN 資料同聯絡資料。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？
**Model:** Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, apart from one record I still need to find.
**Model:** 有呀，不過仲有一份紀錄我要再搵吓。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用商業網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。
**Model:** Use the business online portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** After I lodge the information, what should I do while they assess it? I don’t want to create another problem.
**Model:** 我正式交咗資料之後，佢哋評估嗰陣咁我應該點做呀？我唔想又整多個問題出嚟。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 一般參考時間係大約七至十個工作日。等候期間，任何更改確認之前只使用已核實嘅商業資料，並留意任何正式訊息。
**Model:** The usual guide is about seven to ten business days. While you wait, keep using only verified business details until any change is confirmed, and monitor any official messages.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I get a negative decision, can I get the reasons in writing before I decide what to do next?
**Model:** 如果我收到一個負面決定，我可唔可以先攞書面理由，再諗下一步？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，要求相關部門以書面核對或者更正紀錄。
**Model:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, ask the agency to check or correct the record in writing.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use registered business name when I contact the service again.
**Model:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返註冊商業名稱呢個詞。

## D003 — GST registration threshold

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’m following up on a GST registration question. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
**Model:** 我係跟進GST 登記問題。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係預計全年營業額係七萬八千澳元。我擔心可能要延遲開業或者營運。
**Model:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that projected annual turnover is $78,000. What worries me is that I may have to delay starting or running the business.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Before deciding the next step, remember the term Goods and Services Tax, or GST. In this type of matter, official business details must match the entity actually trading.
**Model:** 決定下一步之前，要記住商品及服務稅，即係 GST呢個詞。喺呢類事情，正式商業資料要同實際經營嘅實體一致。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果通知所列日期之前都搞唔掂，問題會有幾大呀？
**Model:** How serious is it if I’m not able to fix this before the date shown on the notice?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, submit the correct application or update and keep the confirmation. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
**Model:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，提交正確申請或者更新資料，並保留確認紀錄。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有銷售紀錄同現時預測。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
**Model:** Will sales records and current forecasts be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Have you got those records ready to send?
**Model:** 嗰啲紀錄你而家準備好可以交未？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 有，淨係仲有一份文件要遲啲先拎到。
**Model:** Yes. There’s just one document I need to bring later.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Start with the requested material through the business online portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
**Model:** 先經商業網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我點樣證明自己係邊日交文件，我唔想遲啲先發現原來啲文件根本冇收到。
**Model:** How do I prove what day I sent the documents, I don’t want to find out later that the files never arrived.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Save the confirmation number. Allow about two weeks; during that time, keep using only verified business details until any change is confirmed.
**Model:** 保存網上確認資料。預大約兩星期；期間，任何更改確認之前只使用已核實嘅商業資料。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果到時都仲未有消息，我係咪應該問返而家嗰份申請，定係重新開始？
**Model:** If I still haven’t heard anything by then, do I call about the current application, or start again?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask the agency to check or correct the record in writing.
**Model:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，要求相關部門以書面核對或者更正紀錄。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住商品及服務稅，即係 GST。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Goods and Services Tax, or GST. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That’s all I needed to know. I’ll follow the steps you’ve given me.
**Model:** 我想知嘅就係呢啲喇。我會照你講嘅步驟做。

## D004 — Preparing a tax invoice

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你之前要求我哋解釋客戶發票問題。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。
**Model:** You asked us to explain a customer invoice problem. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. I can see that the invoice total is $1,650 including GST, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.
**Model:** 係。我見到發票總額係一千六百五十澳元，已包括 GST，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我哋而家要睇稅務發票。一般最重要嘅一點係正式商業資料要同實際經營嘅實體一致。我即刻幫你對一對件紀錄。
**Model:** The key expression is tax invoice. The important general point is that official business details must match the entity actually trading.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I need to deal with it before the next scheduled appointment or payment. Otherwise, I’m a bit worried I’ll have to delay starting or running the business.
**Model:** 我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理。否則，我擔心可能要延遲開業或者營運。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你講嗰個金額而家仲準確？
**Model:** Is the amount you mentioned still accurate?

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, the latest message says the same thing.
**Model:** 係，最新訊息都係咁講。

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係提交正確申請或者更新資料，並保留確認紀錄。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。
**Model:** The safest next step is to submit the correct application or update and keep the confirmation. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have sale record and ABN details, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?
**Model:** 我有銷售紀錄同 ABN 資料，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 頭先講嗰啲紀錄你有冇帶嚟？
**Model:** Have you brought the records we just discussed?

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. There’s just one document I need to bring later.
**Model:** 有，淨係仲有一份文件要遲啲先拎到。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該。經商業網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the business online portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** During the assessment, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?
**Model:** 評估期間，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約三至四星期。期間，任何更改確認之前只使用已核實嘅商業資料。
**Model:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, keep using only verified business details until any change is confirmed.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I’m still unhappy after I get the written decision, who should I contact next instead of making the same phone call again?
**Model:** 如果收到書面決定之後我都仲唔滿意，我下一步應該搵邊個，先唔使又打同一輪電話？

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，要求相關部門以書面核對或者更正紀錄。
**Model:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask the agency to check or correct the record in writing.

### Segment 16 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of tax invoice with the reference number for future contact.
**Model:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將稅務發票同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。

## D005 — Lodging a BAS

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Let’s check the progress of a quarterly business activity statement. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.
**Model:** 我哋睇下每季業務活動報表嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 之前有人話該季度喺六月三十日完結。我仍然擔心可能要延遲開業或者營運。
**Model:** Earlier I was told the quarter ended on 30 June. I’m still concerned that I may have to delay starting or running the business.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** For this matter, we need to be clear about Business Activity Statement, or BAS. Remember that official business details must match the entity actually trading. Let me check how that applies to you.
**Model:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係業務活動報表，即係 BAS。要留意正式商業資料要同實際經營嘅實體一致。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我呢邊而家係咪齊晒？我唔想過咗下星期五先知有嘢漏咗。
**Model:** Is my side complete now? I don’t want to discover something is missing after next Friday.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The next step is to submit the correct application or update and keep the confirmation. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
**Model:** 下一步係提交正確申請或者更新資料，並保留確認紀錄。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗銷售、GST 同開支紀錄。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
**Model:** I have sales, GST and expense records. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Use the business online portal, then keep the submission receipt. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
**Model:** 可以。用商業網上平台提交，之後留返提交收據。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 啲文件交齊之後，我應該等幾耐先再打電話？
**Model:** Once the documents are in, how long should I leave it before I call?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about five business days. During that period, keep using only verified business details until any change is confirmed, and check the official contact channel regularly.
**Model:** 預大約五個工作日。期間，任何更改確認之前只使用已核實嘅商業資料，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果未有決定之前我嘅聯絡資料變咗，我係咪應該即刻通知佢哋？
**Model:** What should I do if my contact details change before there’s a decision, should I tell them straight away?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, ask the agency to check or correct the record in writing.
**Model:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，要求相關部門以書面核對或者更正紀錄。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低業務活動報表，即係 BAS，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
**Model:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down Business Activity Statement, or BAS so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D006 — Changing to a company

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 早晨。呢度係小型企業顧問。我聯絡你係關於由獨資經營轉做公司。多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。
**Model:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a small-business adviser. I’m calling about a change from sole trader to company. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What worries me is that I may have to delay starting or running the business. I’d like this resolved before the end of this month.
**Model:** 我有啲驚會要延遲開業或者營運。我想喺今個月底之前處理好。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢件事最緊要先搞清楚私人有限公司，即係 Pty Ltd。正式商業資料要同實際經營嘅實體一致。我幫你睇吓你個情況實際點樣套用。
**Model:** This comes down to proprietary limited company, or Pty Ltd. official business details must match the entity actually trading. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My paperwork says the company is due to start on 1 October. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?
**Model:** 我份文件寫住公司預計十月一日開始運作。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係提交正確申請或者更新資料，並保留確認紀錄。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。
**Model:** The record matches that detail. Next, submit the correct application or update and keep the confirmation. Check every date and number before submitting.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have company registration and banking details. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗公司註冊同銀行資料。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用商業網上平台。上載公司註冊同銀行資料，之後保存收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the business online portal. Upload company registration and banking details, then save the receipt or reference.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** How long should I wait? I’m a bit worried I’ll have to delay starting or running the business. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?
**Model:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能要延遲開業或者營運。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約七至十個工作日。期間，任何更改確認之前只使用已核實嘅商業資料。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。
**Model:** Allow about seven to ten business days. Meanwhile, keep using only verified business details until any change is confirmed. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the result still looks wrong to me, should I ask for written reasons first, or request a review straight away?
**Model:** 如果個結果我點睇都覺得唔對路，我應該先攞書面理由，定係即刻申請覆核？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，要求相關部門以書面核對或者更正紀錄。
**Model:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask the agency to check or correct the record in writing.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks for explaining it. I’ll wait for the update and keep the records together.
**Model:** 唔該你解釋。我會等更新，同時將啲紀錄整理好。

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 明白。我會留返確認紀錄，有需要先再跟進。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the confirmation and follow up only if I need to.

## D007 — Market stall permit

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks for calling a small-business adviser. I can see an enquiry about a weekend market-stall application. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
**Model:** 多謝你打嚟小型企業顧問。我見到有一個關於週末市集攤位申請嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到臨時食品攤位許可證。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
**Model:** I received an update today. It mentions temporary food stall permit, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That term matters because official business details must match the entity actually trading. Your current record also says the first market is on 12 September.
**Model:** 呢個詞重要，因為正式商業資料要同實際經營嘅實體一致。你目前紀錄亦顯示第一個市集係九月十二日。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺通知所列日期之前知道，因為可能要延遲開業或者營運。
**Model:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the date shown on the notice, because I may have to delay starting or running the business.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, submit the correct application or update and keep the confirmation.
**Model:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要提交正確申請或者更新資料，並保留確認紀錄。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗公眾責任保險同食品安全資料。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
**Model:** I’ve gathered public-liability and food-safety details. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Can you show me the documents you’ve gathered so far?
**Model:** 你可唔可以畀我睇吓而家已經準備好嘅資料？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 主要文件準備好喇，遲啲我會再補多一份。
**Model:** The main documents are ready. I’ll add one more later.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Use the business online portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
**Model:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用商業網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我交咗啲文件之後，等緊期間有冇啲嘢一定要做或者唔好做呀？
**Model:** Once I’ve submitted the documents, is there anything I should or shouldn’t do while I’m waiting?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The usual guide is about two weeks. While you wait, keep using only verified business details until any change is confirmed, and monitor any official messages.
**Model:** 一般參考時間係大約兩星期。等候期間，任何更改確認之前只使用已核實嘅商業資料，並留意任何正式訊息。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果個申請畀人拒絕，我可唔可以先攞書面理由，再諗下一步？
**Model:** If the application is refused, can I get the reasons in writing before I decide what to do next?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, ask the agency to check or correct the record in writing.
**Model:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，要求相關部門以書面核對或者更正紀錄。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返臨時食品攤位許可證呢個詞。
**Model:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use temporary food stall permit when I contact the service again.

## D008 — Supplier credit terms

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我係跟進供應商戶口申請。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。
**Model:** I’m following up on a supplier account application. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that the first order is worth $4,800. My main worry is that I could have to delay starting or running the business.
**Model:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係第一張訂單價值四千八百澳元。我擔心可能要延遲開業或者營運。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 決定下一步之前，要記住三十日商業付款期呢個詞。喺呢類事情，商業信貸付款期會視乎供應商嘅賬戶政策同信貸審批。
**Model:** Before deciding the next step, remember the term thirty-day trading terms. In this type of matter, commercial credit terms depend on the supplier’s account policy and credit approval.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the next scheduled appointment or payment arrives and this is still unresolved, what should I expect?
**Model:** 去到下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期件事仲未解決，我應該預咩情況？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，完成信貸戶口申請並提供所需商業推薦資料。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。
**Model:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, complete the credit-account application and provide the requested business references. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Will business references and bank details be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?
**Model:** 我有商業推薦資料同銀行資料。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我哋要嗰幾份文件而家喺唔喺你面前？
**Model:** Do you have the documents we need in front of you?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have most of them; one is still at home.
**Model:** 大部分有呀，不過仲有一份喺屋企。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 先經供應商賬戶網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。
**Model:** Start with the requested material through the supplier account portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Is there a receipt or reference after I submit it, That date could matter if there’s a dispute later.
**Model:** 我交咗之後會唔會有收據或者參考編號，如果之後有爭議，嗰個日期可能好重要㗎。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 記低提交參考編號。預大約三至四星期；期間，信貸正式批核之前，每張訂單都要確認付款條款。
**Model:** Keep the electronic acknowledgement. Allow about three to four weeks; during that time, confirm the payment terms for each order until credit approval is final.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Suppose I’m still waiting after that, do I call about the current application, or start again?
**Model:** 假如到時我仲係等緊，我係咪應該問返而家嗰份申請，定係重新開始？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，要求供應商賬戶部解釋信貸決定或者可用付款條款。
**Model:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask the supplier accounts team to explain the credit decision or available payment terms.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Great, I’ll take care of that and contact you again if something changes.
**Model:** 好呀，我會搞掂佢。有咩改動我再聯絡你哋。

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔該你解釋。我會等更新，同時將啲紀錄整理好。
**Model:** Thanks for explaining it. I’ll wait for the update and keep the records together.

## D009 — Faulty refrigerator

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** You asked us to explain a complaint about a faulty refrigerator. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
**Model:** 你之前要求我哋解釋雪櫃故障投訴。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係。我見到一千八百九十九澳元嘅雪櫃用咗六星期就壞，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
**Model:** Yes. I can see that the $1,899 refrigerator failed after six weeks, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The key expression is consumer guarantee. The important general point is that consumer guarantees and contract terms can affect the available remedy.
**Model:** 呢個情況主要關乎消費者保障。一般最重要嘅一點係消費者保障同合約條款會影響可以要求嘅補救。不如我哋先核對你而家嘅資料，再決定下一步。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我需要喺下星期五之前處理。否則，我擔心可能會有金錢損失，或者錯過要求補救嘅機會。
**Model:** I need to deal with it before next Friday. Otherwise, I’m anxious that I may lose money or miss the chance to obtain a remedy.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Before I note it down, is that detail definitely current?
**Model:** 我記低之前想確認，嗰個資料真係最新？

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我收到最新嘅紀錄就係呢份。
**Model:** That’s the newest record I’ve received.

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The safest next step is to write to the trader, explain the problem and request an appropriate remedy. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.
**Model:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係書面聯絡商戶，解釋問題同要求合適補救。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有收據、相片同維修報告，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
**Model:** I have receipt, photos and service report, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Do you have copies of those records with you?
**Model:** 嗰啲紀錄你有冇副本喺身？

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 主要文件準備好喇，遲啲我會再補多一份。
**Model:** The main documents are ready. I’ll add one more later.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the trader’s customer portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
**Model:** 應該。經商戶客戶網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 等佢哋作決定之前，仲有冇其他情況我應該早啲有準備？
**Model:** Until they make a decision, could anything else happen that I need to prepare for?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about five business days. Meanwhile, keep the item, receipt and all written messages.
**Model:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約五個工作日。期間，保留貨品、收據同所有書面訊息。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果啲理由同實際發生嘅事對唔上，我下一步應該搵邊個，先唔使又打同一輪電話？
**Model:** If the reasons don’t match what actually happened, who should I contact next instead of making the same phone call again?

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then write to the trader first, then contact the relevant state consumer service if needed.
**Model:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，先書面聯絡商戶，有需要再聯絡所在州嘅消費者服務。

### Segment 16 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將消費者保障同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
**Model:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of consumer guarantee with the reference number for future contact.

## D010 — Used-car dealer dispute

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我哋睇下二手車商投訴嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？
**Model:** Let’s check the progress of a used-car complaint. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Earlier I was told the car cost $16,500 three weeks ago. I’m still concerned that I may lose money or miss the chance to obtain a remedy.
**Model:** 之前有人話架車三星期前買入，價錢一萬六千五百澳元。我仍然擔心可能會有金錢損失，或者錯過要求補救嘅機會。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係嚴重故障。要留意消費者保障同合約條款會影響可以要求嘅補救。
**Model:** This comes down to major failure. Remember that consumer guarantees and contract terms can affect the available remedy. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Do you need anything else from me now, rather than after the end of this month?
**Model:** 有冇其他資料想我而家交，而唔係等過咗今個月底先補？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 下一步係書面聯絡商戶，解釋問題同要求合適補救。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。
**Model:** The next step is to write to the trader, explain the problem and request an appropriate remedy. The service can then check whether anything further is required.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have sale contract and repair invoice. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗買賣合約同維修發票。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。用商戶客戶網上平台提交，之後保存確認編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。
**Model:** Yes. Use the trader’s customer portal, then keep the submission receipt. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** After I submit it, how long should I leave it before I call?
**Model:** 我交咗之後，我應該等幾耐先再打電話？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約七至十個工作日。期間，保留貨品、收據同所有書面訊息，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。
**Model:** Allow about seven to ten business days. During that period, keep the item, receipt and all written messages, and check the official contact channel regularly.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Suppose one of my details changes during the assessment, do I update the current request or wait for the decision first?
**Model:** 假如評估期間我有一項資料改咗，我係咪更新返而家個申請，定係等有決定先？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，先書面聯絡商戶，有需要再聯絡所在州嘅消費者服務。
**Model:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, write to the trader first, then contact the relevant state consumer service if needed.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down major failure so I can refer to the matter accurately.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低嚴重故障，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。

## D011 — Online order not delivered

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a consumer information officer. I’m calling about a missing online order. I can see the notes from your enquiry. What’s the part you’re most concerned about?
**Model:** 早晨。呢度係消費者事務資訊主任。我聯絡你係關於未收到嘅網購訂單。我睇到你之前個查詢紀錄。你而家最擔心係邊一部分呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 而家我最憂心係可能會有金錢損失，或者錯過要求補救嘅機會。我想喺通知所列日期之前處理好。
**Model:** I’m concerned I might lose money or miss the chance to obtain a remedy. I’d like this resolved before the date shown on the notice.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** This comes down to proof of purchase. consumer guarantees and contract terms can affect the available remedy. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.
**Model:** 而家要睇嘅重點係購買證明。消費者保障同合約條款會影響可以要求嘅補救。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我份文件寫住七月二十八日已付款四百二十澳元。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
**Model:** My paperwork says $420 was paid on 28 July. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The record matches that detail. Next, write to the trader, explain the problem and request an appropriate remedy. Check every date and number before submitting.
**Model:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係書面聯絡商戶，解釋問題同要求合適補救。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗訂單確認同付款紀錄。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
**Model:** I have order confirmation and payment record. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the trader’s customer portal. Upload order confirmation and payment record, then save the receipt or reference.
**Model:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用商戶客戶網上平台。上載訂單確認同付款紀錄，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能會有金錢損失，或者錯過要求補救嘅機會。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
**Model:** How long should I wait? I’m a bit worried I’ll lose money or miss the chance to obtain a remedy. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, keep the item, receipt and all written messages. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
**Model:** 預大約兩星期。期間，保留貨品、收據同所有書面訊息。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我懷疑個機構搞錯咗，我應該先攞書面理由，定係即刻申請覆核？
**Model:** If I believe the service has made a mistake, should I ask for written reasons first, or request a review straight away?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, write to the trader first, then contact the relevant state consumer service if needed.
**Model:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，先書面聯絡商戶，有需要再聯絡所在州嘅消費者服務。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔該，咁就清楚喇。我會留返確認紀錄，之後要跟進都有得查。
**Model:** Thanks, that clears it up. I’ll keep the confirmation in case I need to come back to it.

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That’s all I needed to know. I’ll follow the steps you’ve given me.
**Model:** 我想知嘅就係呢啲喇。我會照你講嘅步驟做。

## D012 — Gym cancellation

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝你打嚟消費者事務資訊主任。我見到有一個關於健身中心會籍取消嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。
**Model:** Thanks for calling a consumer information officer. I can see an enquiry about a gym membership cancellation. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I received an update today. It mentions cancellation terms, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.
**Model:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到取消條款。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個詞重要，因為消費者保障同合約條款會影響可以要求嘅補救。你目前紀錄亦顯示琴日又扣咗八十九澳元。
**Model:** That term matters because consumer guarantees and contract terms can affect the available remedy. Your current record also says another $89 was debited yesterday.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the next scheduled appointment or payment, because I may lose money or miss the chance to obtain a remedy.
**Model:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前知道，因為可能會有金錢損失，或者錯過要求補救嘅機會。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要書面聯絡商戶，解釋問題同要求合適補救。
**Model:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, write to the trader, explain the problem and request an appropriate remedy.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve gathered membership contract and cancellation email. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗會籍合約同取消電郵。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你今日方唔方便畀我睇嗰啲紀錄？
**Model:** Are you able to show me those records today?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve got nearly everything, but one item isn’t with me.
**Model:** 差唔多全部有，不過仲有一樣冇帶喺身。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用商戶客戶網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。
**Model:** Use the trader’s customer portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Once I’ve submitted the documents, is there anything I should or shouldn’t do while I’m waiting?
**Model:** 我交咗啲文件之後，等緊期間有冇啲嘢一定要做或者唔好做呀？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 一般參考時間係大約三至四星期。等候期間，保留貨品、收據同所有書面訊息，並留意任何正式訊息。
**Model:** The usual guide is about three to four weeks. While you wait, keep the item, receipt and all written messages, and monitor any official messages.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I get a negative decision, will they tell me what part of the application caused the problem?
**Model:** 如果我收到一個負面決定，佢哋會唔會講明係申請邊一部分出問題？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，先書面聯絡商戶，有需要再聯絡所在州嘅消費者服務。
**Model:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, write to the trader first, then contact the relevant state consumer service if needed.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** All right, I know what to do now. I’ll keep a copy of whatever I send.
**Model:** 好，而家我知點做喇。我交出去嘅資料都會留返副本。

## D013 — Mobile plan billing

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’m following up on a mobile-plan billing dispute. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
**Model:** 我係跟進手機計劃賬單爭議。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係今個月爭議收費係六十五澳元。我擔心可能會有金錢損失，或者錯過要求補救嘅機會。
**Model:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that the disputed charge is $65 this month. What worries me is that I may lose money or miss the chance to obtain a remedy.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** This comes down to itemised bill. In this type of matter, consumer guarantees and contract terms can affect the available remedy. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.
**Model:** 決定下一步之前，要記住分項賬單呢個詞。喺呢類事情，消費者保障同合約條款會影響可以要求嘅補救。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果下星期五之前都搞唔掂，問題會有幾大呀？
**Model:** How serious is it if I’m not able to fix this before next Friday?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, write to the trader, explain the problem and request an appropriate remedy. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
**Model:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，書面聯絡商戶，解釋問題同要求合適補救。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有合約摘要同最近賬單。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
**Model:** Will contract summary and recent bills be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Can you pull up those documents on your phone?
**Model:** 你可唔可以而家用電話開返啲文件？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 重要嗰啲喺晒度，淨係欠一份證明。
**Model:** The important ones are here. I’m missing one supporting record.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Start with the requested material through the trader’s customer portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
**Model:** 先經商戶客戶網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我正式提交之後，點知系統已經有紀錄，我唔想遲啲先發現原來啲文件根本冇收到。
**Model:** Once I lodge it, how will I know it’s on their system, I don’t want to find out later that the files never arrived.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Save the online confirmation. Allow about five business days; during that time, keep the item, receipt and all written messages.
**Model:** 留返電子確認紀錄。預大約五個工作日；期間，保留貨品、收據同所有書面訊息。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果過咗嗰個時間都冇更新，我應該跟返原本個申請，定係再交一份？
**Model:** If there’s no update after that period, should I follow up the existing request or submit another one?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, write to the trader first, then contact the relevant state consumer service if needed.
**Model:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，先書面聯絡商戶，有需要再聯絡所在州嘅消費者服務。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住分項賬單。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember itemised bill. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good, I’ll do that. If anything changes, I’ll let the service know straight away.
**Model:** 好呀，我會照做。如果有任何改動，我會即刻通知返個機構。

## D014 — Renovation quote dispute

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你之前要求我哋解釋家居裝修賬單。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。
**Model:** You asked us to explain a home-renovation bill. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. I can see that the quote was $9,500 but the invoice is $12,300, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.
**Model:** 係。我見到報價九千五百澳元，但發票係一萬二千三百澳元，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個情況主要關乎書面報價。一般最重要嘅一點係消費者保障同合約條款會影響可以要求嘅補救。不如我哋先核對你而家嘅資料，再決定下一步。
**Model:** The key expression is written quote. The important general point is that consumer guarantees and contract terms can affect the available remedy.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I need to deal with it before the end of this month. Otherwise, What worries me is that I could lose money or miss the chance to obtain a remedy.
**Model:** 我需要喺今個月底之前處理。否則，我擔心可能會有金錢損失，或者錯過要求補救嘅機會。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我確認一下，嗰個日期而家仲啱？
**Model:** Can I confirm that date is still correct?

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That’s right. I don’t have anything more recent.
**Model:** 啱，我手上冇再新啲嘅資料。

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係書面聯絡商戶，解釋問題同要求合適補救。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。
**Model:** The safest next step is to write to the trader, explain the problem and request an appropriate remedy. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have quote, invoice and messages, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?
**Model:** 我有報價、發票同訊息紀錄，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你可唔可以而家用電話開返啲文件？
**Model:** Can you pull up those documents on your phone?

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have the main records. One document still needs to be downloaded.
**Model:** 主要紀錄有齊，仲有一份文件要下載返。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該。經商戶客戶網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the trader’s customer portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** While they’re checking it, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?
**Model:** 佢哋核對緊嗰陣，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約七至十個工作日。期間，保留貨品、收據同所有書面訊息。
**Model:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about seven to ten business days. Meanwhile, keep the item, receipt and all written messages.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I read the decision and still think it’s wrong, what’s the proper next step? I don’t want to keep ringing different people.
**Model:** 如果封決定信我睇完都覺得有錯，正確下一步係咩？我唔想不停打去唔同地方。

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，先書面聯絡商戶，有需要再聯絡所在州嘅消費者服務。
**Model:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then write to the trader first, then contact the relevant state consumer service if needed.

### Segment 16 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of written quote with the reference number for future contact.
**Model:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將書面報價同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。

## D015 — Cancelled travel service

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Let’s check the progress of a refund for a cancelled tour. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
**Model:** 我哋睇下取消旅行團後嘅退款嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 之前有人話已付一千二百澳元訂金。我仍然擔心可能會有金錢損失，或者錯過要求補救嘅機會。
**Model:** Earlier I was told a $1,200 deposit was paid. I’m still concerned that I may lose money or miss the chance to obtain a remedy.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What we need to look at is refund entitlement. Remember that consumer guarantees and contract terms can affect the available remedy. I’ll check the details on your case now.
**Model:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係退款權益。要留意消費者保障同合約條款會影響可以要求嘅補救。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我呢邊而家係咪齊晒？我唔想過咗通知所列日期先知有嘢漏咗。
**Model:** Is my side complete now? I don’t want to discover something is missing after the date shown on the notice.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The next step is to write to the trader, explain the problem and request an appropriate remedy. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
**Model:** 下一步係書面聯絡商戶，解釋問題同要求合適補救。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗預訂條款同取消通知。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
**Model:** I have booking terms and cancellation notice. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Use the trader’s customer portal, then note the lodgement reference. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
**Model:** 可以。用商戶客戶網上平台提交，之後記低提交參考編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 上載完成之後，大概幾時再問吓會比較合理？
**Model:** After the upload is complete, what’s a sensible time to ask for an update?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about two weeks. During that period, keep the item, receipt and all written messages, and check the official contact channel regularly.
**Model:** 預大約兩星期。期間，保留貨品、收據同所有書面訊息，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我等緊期間有重要資料改咗，我係咪應該即刻通知佢哋？
**Model:** What if something important changes while I’m waiting, should I tell them straight away?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, write to the trader first, then contact the relevant state consumer service if needed.
**Model:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，先書面聯絡商戶，有需要再聯絡所在州嘅消費者服務。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低退款權益，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
**Model:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down refund entitlement so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D016 — Electricity account dispute

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 早晨。呢度係消費者事務資訊主任。我聯絡你係關於異常高嘅電費賬單。多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。
**Model:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a consumer information officer. I’m calling about an unusually high electricity bill. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’m concerned I might lose money or miss the chance to obtain a remedy. I’d like this resolved before the next scheduled appointment or payment.
**Model:** 我有啲驚會會有金錢損失，或者錯過要求補救嘅機會。我想喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理好。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 而家要睇嘅重點係電錶讀數。消費者保障同合約條款會影響可以要求嘅補救。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。
**Model:** This comes down to meter reading. consumer guarantees and contract terms can affect the available remedy. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My paperwork says the quarterly bill is $740. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?
**Model:** 我份文件寫住今季賬單係七百四十澳元。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係書面聯絡商戶，解釋問題同要求合適補救。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。
**Model:** The record matches that detail. Next, write to the trader, explain the problem and request an appropriate remedy. Check every date and number before submitting.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have bill and current meter photo. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗賬單同最新電錶相片。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用商戶客戶網上平台。上載賬單同最新電錶相片，之後保存收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the trader’s customer portal. Upload bill and current meter photo, then save the receipt or reference.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** How long should I wait? My worry is that I might lose money or miss the chance to obtain a remedy. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?
**Model:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能會有金錢損失，或者錯過要求補救嘅機會。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約三至四星期。期間，保留貨品、收據同所有書面訊息。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。
**Model:** Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, keep the item, receipt and all written messages. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I believe the service has made a mistake, can I ask for a review before making a complaint?
**Model:** 如果我懷疑個機構搞錯咗，我可唔可以先要求覆核，再決定使唔使投訴呀？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，先書面聯絡商戶，有需要再聯絡所在州嘅消費者服務。
**Model:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, write to the trader first, then contact the relevant state consumer service if needed.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.
**Model:** 好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔該晒。我會照住啲步驟做，之後收到新通知亦會睇清楚。
**Model:** Thanks for your help. I’ll follow those steps and check the next notice carefully.

## D017 — Casual loading missing

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks for calling a workplace relations adviser. I can see an enquiry about a casual pay-rate query. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
**Model:** 多謝你打嚟僱傭關係顧問。我見到有一個關於臨時僱員工資率查詢嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到臨時僱員附加薪酬。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
**Model:** I received an update today. It mentions casual loading, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That term matters because minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type. Your current record also says the base rate shown is $25.40 an hour.
**Model:** 呢個詞重要，因為最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。你目前紀錄亦顯示顯示嘅基本時薪係二十五個四毫澳元。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下星期五之前知道，因為工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。
**Model:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before next Friday, because my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing.
**Model:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗更表、合約同糧單。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
**Model:** I’ve gathered roster, contract and pay slip. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Did you manage to bring the supporting documents?
**Model:** 你最後有冇帶到啲證明文件嚟？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 大部分證明都帶咗，仲有一樣喺第二度。
**Model:** I’ve got most of the evidence with me; one item is elsewhere.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Use the employee self-service portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
**Model:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用僱員自助網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我正式交咗資料之後，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
**Model:** After I lodge the information, what happens while the matter is being checked?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The usual guide is about five business days. While you wait, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips, and monitor any official messages.
**Model:** 一般參考時間係大約五個工作日。等候期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單，並留意任何正式訊息。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果個申請畀人拒絕，我可唔可以先攞書面理由，再諗下一步？
**Model:** If the application is refused, can I get the reasons in writing before I decide what to do next?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.
**Model:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返臨時僱員附加薪酬呢個詞。
**Model:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use casual loading when I contact the service again.

## D018 — Weekend penalty rates

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我係跟進週末更份薪酬。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。
**Model:** I’m following up on weekend shift payments. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that fourteen weekend hours were worked last pay period. My concern is that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly.
**Model:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係上一個出糧期做咗十四個週末鐘。我擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 決定下一步之前，要記住特別時段附加工資呢個詞。喺呢類事情，最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。
**Model:** Before deciding the next step, remember the term penalty rates. In this type of matter, minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the end of this month arrives and this is still unresolved, what should I expect?
**Model:** 去到今個月底件事仲未解決，我應該預咩情況？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。
**Model:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Will time sheet, roster and pay slip be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?
**Model:** 我有工時表、更表同糧單。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 嗰啲紀錄你而家準備好可以交未？
**Model:** Have you got those records ready to send?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The important ones are here. I’m missing one supporting record.
**Model:** 重要嗰啲喺晒度，淨係欠一份證明。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 先經僱員自助網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。
**Model:** Start with the requested material through the employee self-service portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Will I get anything showing the upload went through, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.
**Model:** 我上載完會唔會有嘢證明成功咗，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 記低提交參考編號。預大約七至十個工作日；期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單。
**Model:** Save the online confirmation. Allow about seven to ten business days; during that time, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I still haven’t heard anything by then, do I call about the current application, or start again?
**Model:** 如果到時都仲未有消息，我係咪應該問返而家嗰份申請，定係重新開始？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。
**Model:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember penalty rates. That should help me explain the issue clearly.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住特別時段附加工資。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 咁就明喇。我會補返欠嗰份資料，再留低個參考編號。
**Model:** That makes sense. I’ll sort out the missing item and keep the reference number.

## D019 — Award classification

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** You asked us to explain a job classification. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
**Model:** 你之前要求我哋解釋職位分類。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係。我見到已經做咗三個月主管職務，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
**Model:** Yes. I can see that supervisor duties have been done for three months, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The key expression is modern award classification. The important general point is that minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type.
**Model:** 而家要睇嘅重點係現代 award 職級分類。一般最重要嘅一點係最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我需要喺通知所列日期之前處理。否則，我擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。
**Model:** I need to deal with it before the date shown on the notice. Otherwise, I’m worried that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** So we’re both looking at the same current detail?
**Model:** 即係我哋兩邊而家睇緊同一個最新資料？

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係呀，我啲文件而家都仲係咁寫。
**Model:** Yes, that’s still what my paperwork shows.

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The safest next step is to ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.
**Model:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有職位說明同最近更表，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
**Model:** I have job description and recent rosters, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Are the supporting documents handy at the moment?
**Model:** 啲證明文件而家喺唔喺手邊？

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 有呀，不過仲有一份紀錄我要再搵吓。
**Model:** Yes, apart from one record I still need to find.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the employee self-service portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
**Model:** 應該。經僱員自助網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 件事仲處理緊嗰陣，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？
**Model:** While this is still being sorted out, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips.
**Model:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約兩星期。期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果啲理由同實際發生嘅事對唔上，我下一步應該搵邊個，先唔使又打同一輪電話？
**Model:** If the reasons don’t match what actually happened, who should I contact next instead of making the same phone call again?

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.
**Model:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。

### Segment 16 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將現代 award 職級分類同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
**Model:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of modern award classification with the reference number for future contact.

## D020 — Annual leave request

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我哋睇下年假申請嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我搵到你個查詢喇。講細節之前，你最想解決嘅係咩呀？
**Model:** Let’s check the progress of an annual-leave request. I’ve found the enquiry. Before we get into the details, tell me what you need resolved.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Earlier I was told ten days were requested from 5 October. I’m still concerned that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly.
**Model:** 之前有人話申請十月五日起放十日假。我仍然擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係年假結餘。要留意最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。
**Model:** This comes down to annual leave balance. Remember that minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Do you need anything else from me now, rather than after the next scheduled appointment or payment?
**Model:** 有冇其他資料想我而家交，而唔係等過咗下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期先補？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 下一步係以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。
**Model:** The next step is to ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing. The service can then check whether anything further is required.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have leave request and current balance. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗假期申請同現時結餘。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。用僱員自助網上平台提交，之後保存網上確認資料。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。
**Model:** Yes. Use the employee self-service portal, then save the online confirmation. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Once they have everything, what’s a sensible time to ask for an update?
**Model:** 佢哋收齊資料之後，大概幾時再問吓會比較合理？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約三至四星期。期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。
**Model:** Allow about three to four weeks. During that period, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips, and check the official contact channel regularly.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What if something important changes while I’m waiting, how do I make sure the new information is attached to this matter?
**Model:** 如果我等緊期間有重要資料改咗，點樣先可以確保新資料加返落同一件事度？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。
**Model:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down annual leave balance so I can refer to the matter accurately.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低年假結餘，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。

## D021 — Personal leave evidence

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a workplace relations adviser. I’m calling about a recent sick-leave absence. I’ve found the enquiry. Before we get into the details, tell me what you need resolved.
**Model:** 早晨。呢度係僱傭關係顧問。我聯絡你係關於最近病假缺勤。我搵到你個查詢喇。講細節之前，你最想解決嘅係咩呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我最擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。我想喺下星期五之前處理好。
**Model:** My concern is that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly. I’d like this resolved before next Friday.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What we need to look at is personal or sick leave. minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type. I’ll check the details on your case now.
**Model:** 而家要睇嘅重點係個人假或者病假。最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我份文件寫住今個星期缺席咗兩更。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
**Model:** My paperwork says two shifts were missed this week. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The record matches that detail. Next, ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing. Check every date and number before submitting.
**Model:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗醫生證明同請假表。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
**Model:** I have medical certificate and leave form. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the employee self-service portal. Upload medical certificate and leave form, then save the receipt or reference.
**Model:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用僱員自助網上平台。上載醫生證明同請假表，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
**Model:** How long should I wait? I’m worried that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about five business days. Meanwhile, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
**Model:** 預大約五個工作日。期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果個結果我點睇都覺得唔對路，我應該先攞書面理由，定係即刻申請覆核？
**Model:** If the result still looks wrong to me, should I ask for written reasons first, or request a review straight away?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.
**Model:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好。我攞到欠嗰份文件就補返，到時再跟住處理。
**Model:** Okay. I’ll bring the missing document when I have it and we can go from there.

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That’s all I needed to know. I’ll follow the steps you’ve given me.
**Model:** 我想知嘅就係呢啲喇。我會照你講嘅步驟做。

## D022 — Unpaid superannuation

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝你打嚟僱傭關係顧問。我見到有一個關於欠交退休金供款嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。
**Model:** Thanks for calling a workplace relations adviser. I can see an enquiry about missing super contributions. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I received an update today. It mentions superannuation guarantee contribution, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.
**Model:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到法定退休金供款。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個詞重要，因為最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。你目前紀錄亦顯示缺少嘅期間係四月至六月。
**Model:** That term matters because minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type. Your current record also says the missing period is April to June.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the end of this month, because my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly.
**Model:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺今個月底之前知道，因為工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。
**Model:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve gathered pay slips and super-fund statement. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗糧單同退休金基金結單。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 嗰啲紀錄你有冇副本喺身？
**Model:** Do you have copies of those records with you?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, apart from one record I still need to find.
**Model:** 有呀，不過仲有一份紀錄我要再搵吓。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用僱員自助網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。
**Model:** Use the employee self-service portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I send everything today, is there anything else I need to watch while it’s under assessment?
**Model:** 如果我今日交齊啲資料，評估期間仲有冇啲咩要特別留意？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 一般參考時間係大約七至十個工作日。等候期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單，並留意任何正式訊息。
**Model:** The usual guide is about seven to ten business days. While you wait, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips, and monitor any official messages.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the application is refused, can I get the reasons in writing before I decide what to do next?
**Model:** 如果個申請畀人拒絕，我可唔可以先攞書面理由，再諗下一步？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。
**Model:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use superannuation guarantee contribution when I contact the service again.
**Model:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返法定退休金供款呢個詞。

## D023 — Workplace injury report

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’m following up on a workplace injury. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
**Model:** 我係跟進工傷。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係星期二返工時整傷手腕。我擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。
**Model:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that a wrist was injured during Tuesday’s shift. My concern is that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What we need to look at is incident report. In this type of matter, minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type. I’ll check the details on your case now.
**Model:** 決定下一步之前，要記住事故報告呢個詞。喺呢類事情，最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果到通知所列日期都未搞掂，會點㗎？我驚會錯過下一步。
**Model:** If this isn’t sorted out by the date shown on the notice, what happens? I’m worried I’ll miss the next step.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
**Model:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有醫生證明同事故詳情。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
**Model:** Will medical certificate and incident details be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Can you show me the supporting records today?
**Model:** 你今日可唔可以畀我睇吓啲證明資料？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 大部分都有副本，仲欠一份資料。
**Model:** I have copies of most of them; one is still outstanding.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Start with the requested material through the employee self-service portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
**Model:** 先經僱員自助網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我上載完會唔會有嘢證明成功咗，如果之後有爭議，嗰個日期可能好重要㗎。
**Model:** Will I get anything showing the upload went through, That date could matter if there’s a dispute later.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Keep the electronic acknowledgement. Allow about two weeks; during that time, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips.
**Model:** 記低提交參考編號。預大約兩星期；期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 假如到時我仲係等緊，我係咪應該問返而家嗰份申請，定係重新開始？
**Model:** Suppose I’m still waiting after that, do I call about the current application, or start again?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.
**Model:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住事故報告。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember incident report. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good, I’ll do that. If anything changes, I’ll let the service know straight away.
**Model:** 好呀，我會照做。如果有任何改動，我會即刻通知返個機構。

## D024 — Unfair dismissal enquiry

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你之前要求我哋解釋最近被解僱。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。
**Model:** You asked us to explain a recent dismissal. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. I can see that employment ended on 7 August, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.
**Model:** 係。我見到僱傭關係喺八月七日終止，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個情況主要關乎不公平解僱申請。一般最重要嘅一點係最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。不如我哋先核對你而家嘅資料，再決定下一步。
**Model:** The key expression is unfair dismissal application. The important general point is that minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I need to deal with it before the next scheduled appointment or payment. Otherwise, I’m worried that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly.
**Model:** 我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理。否則，我擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 繼續之前想核實吓，嗰個資料而家仲有效？
**Model:** Before we move on, is that detail still current?

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Correct. I haven’t received a newer update.
**Model:** 啱，我冇再收到更新資料。

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。
**Model:** The safest next step is to ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have termination letter and employment contract, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?
**Model:** 我有解僱信同僱傭合約，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你今日可唔可以畀我睇吓啲證明資料？
**Model:** Can you show me the supporting records today?

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Most are here; I can send the last one tonight.
**Model:** 大部分都喺度，最後嗰份我今晚可以補交。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該。經僱員自助網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the employee self-service portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Before they finish reviewing the record, could anything else happen that I need to prepare for?
**Model:** 佢哋未核對完份紀錄之前，仲有冇其他情況我應該早啲有準備？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約三至四星期。期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單。
**Model:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the letter doesn’t resolve the problem, what’s the proper next step? I don’t want to keep ringing different people.
**Model:** 如果封信都解決唔到個問題，正確下一步係咩？我唔想不停打去唔同地方。

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。
**Model:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.

### Segment 16 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of unfair dismissal application with the reference number for future contact.
**Model:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將不公平解僱申請同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。

## D025 — Flexible work request

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Let’s check the progress of a flexible-work request. I can see the notes from your enquiry. What’s the part you’re most concerned about?
**Model:** 我哋睇下彈性工作申請嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我睇到你之前個查詢紀錄。你而家最擔心係邊一部分呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 之前有人話申請每星期三日下晝四點收工。我仍然擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。
**Model:** Earlier I was told the request is to finish at 4 pm three days a week. I’m still concerned that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What we need to look at is flexible working arrangement. Remember that minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type. I’ll check the details on your case now.
**Model:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係彈性工作安排。要留意最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果有資料欠咗我想而家補。下星期五之前仲有冇嘢要交？
**Model:** I’d rather fix any missing information now. Is there anything else you need before next Friday?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The next step is to ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
**Model:** 下一步係以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗書面申請同建議更表。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
**Model:** I have written request and proposed roster. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Use the employee self-service portal, then keep the submission receipt. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
**Model:** 可以。用僱員自助網上平台提交，之後保存網上確認資料。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 啲文件交齊之後，通常幾時先適合跟進呀？
**Model:** Once the documents are in, when would it be reasonable to follow up?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about five business days. During that period, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips, and check the official contact channel regularly.
**Model:** 預大約五個工作日。期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果未有決定之前我嘅聯絡資料變咗，我係咪更新返而家個申請，定係等有決定先？
**Model:** What should I do if my contact details change before there’s a decision, do I update the current request or wait for the decision first?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.
**Model:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低彈性工作安排，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
**Model:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down flexible working arrangement so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D026 — Starting a new job

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 早晨。呢度係僱傭關係顧問。我聯絡你係關於新工出糧文件。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？
**Model:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a workplace relations adviser. I’m calling about new-job payroll paperwork. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My concern is that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly. I’d like this resolved before the end of this month.
**Model:** 我最擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。我想喺今個月底之前處理好。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 而家要睇嘅重點係稅務檔案號碼申報。最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。
**Model:** This comes down to Tax File Number declaration. minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My paperwork says the first pay date is 28 August. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?
**Model:** 我份文件寫住第一次出糧日期係八月二十八日。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。
**Model:** The record matches that detail. Next, ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing. Check every date and number before submitting.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have TFN declaration and super choice form. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗TFN 申報同退休金選擇表。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用僱員自助網上平台。上載TFN 申報同退休金選擇表，之後保存收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the employee self-service portal. Upload TFN declaration and super choice form, then save the receipt or reference.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** How long should I wait? I’m worried that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?
**Model:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約七至十個工作日。期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。
**Model:** Allow about seven to ten business days. Meanwhile, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Suppose the decision doesn’t seem right, is there a formal review process I can use?
**Model:** 假如我覺得個決定有問題，係咪有正式嘅覆核程序可以跟？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。
**Model:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.
**Model:** 好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，而家我知點做喇。我交出去嘅資料都會留返副本。
**Model:** All right, I know what to do now. I’ll keep a copy of whatever I send.

## D027 — GP bulk billing

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks for calling a clinic administration officer. I can see an enquiry about a GP appointment fee. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
**Model:** 多謝你打嚟診所行政職員。我見到有一個關於普通科醫生診症費嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到直接向 Medicare 收費。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
**Model:** I received an update today. It mentions bulk billing, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That term matters because Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. Your current record also says the clinic charges $78 with a Medicare rebate available.
**Model:** 呢個詞重要，因為Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。你目前紀錄亦顯示診所收七十八澳元，可以申請 Medicare 回贈。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺通知所列日期之前知道，因為治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
**Model:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the date shown on the notice, because treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing.
