**Model:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗Medicare 卡同預約確認。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
**Model:** I’ve gathered Medicare card and appointment confirmation. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Have you brought copies of the main records?
**Model:** 主要紀錄嘅副本你有冇帶嚟？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 差唔多齊，我今晚可以攞埋欠嗰份。
**Model:** Nearly all. I can get the missing one this evening.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Use the clinic patient portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
**Model:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用診所病人網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我交咗啲文件之後，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
**Model:** Once I’ve submitted the documents, what happens while the matter is being checked?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The usual guide is about two weeks. While you wait, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen, and monitor any official messages.
**Model:** 一般參考時間係大約兩星期。等候期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助，並留意任何正式訊息。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果佢哋最後話唔得，我可唔可以睇咗理由先，再決定使唔使申請覆核？
**Model:** If they eventually say no, can I see the reasons before deciding whether to seek a review?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
**Model:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返直接向 Medicare 收費呢個詞。
**Model:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use bulk billing when I contact the service again.

## D028 — Specialist referral

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我係跟進專科轉介。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。
**Model:** I’m following up on a specialist referral. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that the appointment is booked for 18 November. My concern is that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.
**Model:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係專科預約係十一月十八日。我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 決定下一步之前，要記住專科轉介信呢個詞。喺呢類事情，Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。
**Model:** For this matter, we need to be clear about specialist referral. In this type of matter, Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. Let me check how that applies to you.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** How serious is it if I’m not able to fix this before the next scheduled appointment or payment?
**Model:** 如果下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前都搞唔掂，問題會有幾大呀？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。
**Model:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Will GP referral and specialist booking be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?
**Model:** 我有家庭醫生轉介信同專科預約資料。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 頭先提過嗰啲證明你而家開唔開到？
**Model:** Can you access the evidence we just mentioned?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve got nearly everything, but one item isn’t with me.
**Model:** 差唔多全部有，不過仲有一樣冇帶喺身。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 先經診所病人網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。
**Model:** Start with the requested material through the clinic patient portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Once I lodge it, how will I know it’s on their system, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.
**Model:** 我正式提交之後，點知系統已經有紀錄，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 保存網上確認資料。預大約三至四星期；期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助。
**Model:** Note the lodgement reference. Allow about three to four weeks; during that time, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Suppose I’m still waiting after that, should I ask for a progress update first?
**Model:** 假如到時我仲係等緊，我係咪應該先問吓而家做到邊？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。
**Model:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember specialist referral. That should help me explain the issue clearly.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住專科轉介信。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。
**Model:** Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.

## D029 — Pathology test

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** You asked us to explain a pathology test request. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
**Model:** 你之前要求我哋解釋病理化驗要求。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係。我見到驗血預約係星期一朝早，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
**Model:** Yes. I can see that the blood test is booked for Monday morning, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The key expression is out-of-pocket cost. The important general point is that Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider.
**Model:** 而家要睇嘅重點係自付費用。一般最重要嘅一點係Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我需要喺下星期五之前處理。否則，我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
**Model:** I need to deal with it before next Friday. Otherwise, I’m worried that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** So the figure you gave me is still the one we should use?
**Model:** 咁頭先嗰個數字而家仲係用呢個，係咪？

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 啱呀，如果有改我會再話你知。
**Model:** That’s right. I’ll let you know if it changes.

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The safest next step is to ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.
**Model:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有化驗申請表同 Medicare 卡，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
**Model:** I have request form and Medicare card, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Do you have copies of those records with you?
**Model:** 嗰啲紀錄你有冇副本喺身？

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 大部分有呀，不過仲有一份喺屋企。
**Model:** I have most of them; one is still at home.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the clinic patient portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
**Model:** 應該。經診所病人網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 件事仲處理緊嗰陣，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？
**Model:** While this is still being sorted out, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about five business days. Meanwhile, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen.
**Model:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約五個工作日。期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果封決定信我睇完都覺得有錯，正確下一步係咩？我唔想不停打去唔同地方。
**Model:** If I read the decision and still think it’s wrong, what’s the proper next step? I don’t want to keep ringing different people.

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
**Model:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

### Segment 16 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將自付費用同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
**Model:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of out-of-pocket cost with the reference number for future contact.

## D030 — PBS prescription

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我哋睇下昂貴處方藥嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我搵到你個查詢喇。講細節之前，你最想解決嘅係咩呀？
**Model:** Let’s check the progress of an expensive prescription medicine. I’ve found the enquiry. Before we get into the details, tell me what you need resolved.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Earlier I was told the prescription is for a three-month supply. I’m still concerned that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.
**Model:** 之前有人話處方係三個月份量。我仍然擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係藥物福利計劃，即係 PBS。要留意Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。
**Model:** What we need to look at is Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme, or PBS. Remember that Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. I’ll check the details on your case now.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Do you need anything else from me now, rather than after the end of this month?
**Model:** 有冇其他資料想我而家交，而唔係等過咗今個月底先補？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 下一步係要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。
**Model:** The next step is to ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. The service can then check whether anything further is required.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have prescription and Medicare details. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗處方同 Medicare 資料。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。用診所病人網上平台提交，之後留返電子確認紀錄。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。
**Model:** Yes. Use the clinic patient portal, then keep the electronic acknowledgement. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** After the upload is complete, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?
**Model:** 上載完成之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約七至十個工作日。期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。
**Model:** Allow about seven to ten business days. During that period, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen, and check the official contact channel regularly.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I change address or phone number before they finish, should I tell them straight away?
**Model:** 如果佢哋未處理完之前我搬屋或者換電話，我係咪應該即刻通知佢哋？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。
**Model:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme, or PBS so I can refer to the matter accurately.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低藥物福利計劃，即係 PBS，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。

## D031 — Urgent care clinic

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a clinic administration officer. I’m calling about same-day medical-care options. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
**Model:** 早晨。呢度係診所行政職員。我聯絡你係關於即日醫療選擇。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我最擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。我想喺通知所列日期之前處理好。
**Model:** My concern is that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost. I’d like this resolved before the date shown on the notice.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What we need to look at is Medicare Urgent Care Clinic. Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. I’ll check the details on your case now.
**Model:** 呢件事最緊要先搞清楚Medicare 緊急護理診所。Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。我幫你睇吓你個情況實際點樣套用。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我份文件寫住症狀唔屬於危及生命。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
**Model:** My paperwork says the symptoms are not life-threatening. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The record matches that detail. Next, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. Check every date and number before submitting.
**Model:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗Medicare 卡同現用藥物清單。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
**Model:** I have Medicare card and medication list. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the clinic patient portal. Upload Medicare card and medication list, then save the receipt or reference.
**Model:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用診所病人網上平台。上載Medicare 卡同現用藥物清單，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
**Model:** How long should I wait? I’m worried that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
**Model:** 預大約兩星期。期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我懷疑個機構搞錯咗，我應該搵邊個再睇一次個決定？
**Model:** If I believe the service has made a mistake, who do I ask to look at the decision again?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
**Model:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 明白，我會留意訊息，收到任何新通知都會保存。
**Model:** Right, I’ll keep an eye on the messages and save any new notice I receive.

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. I’ll make sure I keep the receipt and a copy of the documents.
**Model:** 唔該。我會保存收據，同埋留返啲文件副本。

## D032 — Dental treatment quote

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝你打嚟診所行政職員。我見到有一個關於牙科治療報價嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。
**Model:** Thanks for calling a clinic administration officer. I can see an enquiry about a dental treatment estimate. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I received an update today. It mentions treatment plan, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.
**Model:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到治療計劃。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個詞重要，因為Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。你目前紀錄亦顯示建議牙冠治療收一千八百五十澳元。
**Model:** That term matters because Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. Your current record also says the proposed crown costs $1,850.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the next scheduled appointment or payment, because treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.
**Model:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前知道，因為治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。
**Model:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve gathered written quote and health-fund details. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗書面報價同醫療保險資料。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你可唔可以而家用電話開返啲文件？
**Model:** Can you pull up those documents on your phone?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I can show you the main documents; one record is missing.
**Model:** 主要文件我可以畀你睇，仲有一份紀錄未搵到。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用診所病人網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。
**Model:** Use the clinic patient portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** After I lodge the information, is there anything I should or shouldn’t do while I’m waiting?
**Model:** 我正式交咗資料之後，等緊期間有冇啲嘢一定要做或者唔好做呀？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 一般參考時間係大約三至四星期。等候期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助，並留意任何正式訊息。
**Model:** The usual guide is about three to four weeks. While you wait, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen, and monitor any official messages.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If they eventually say no, can I ask them to explain exactly why before I challenge it?
**Model:** 如果佢哋最後話唔得，我可唔可以叫佢哋講清楚點解，再決定係咪覆核？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。
**Model:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use treatment plan when I contact the service again.
**Model:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返治療計劃呢個詞。

## D033 — Physiotherapy sessions

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’m following up on a physiotherapy booking. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
**Model:** 我係跟進物理治療預約。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係每次治療報價一百一十澳元。我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
**Model:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that each session is quoted at $110. My concern is that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Before deciding the next step, remember the term gap payment. In this type of matter, Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider.
**Model:** 決定下一步之前，要記住差額自付費呢個詞。喺呢類事情，Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我喺下星期五之前處理唔到，實際會有咩後果？
**Model:** What are the consequences if I can’t resolve this by next Friday?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
**Model:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有轉介信同保險資料。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
**Model:** Will referral and insurer details be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?
**Model:** 相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 主要文件我可以畀你睇，仲有一份紀錄未搵到。
**Model:** I can show you the main documents; one record is missing.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Start with the requested material through the clinic patient portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
**Model:** 先經診所病人網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我點樣先知佢哋真係收到啲文件，我想有個編號，之後跟進都有嘢可以講。
**Model:** How can I tell they’ve actually received the documents, I want something I can refer to if I need to follow it up.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Save the online confirmation. Allow about five business days; during that time, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen.
**Model:** 保存確認編號。預大約五個工作日；期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果到時都仲未有消息，我係咪應該問返而家嗰份申請，定係重新開始？
**Model:** If I still haven’t heard anything by then, do I call about the current application, or start again?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
**Model:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住差額自付費。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember gap payment. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks, that clears it up. I’ll keep the confirmation in case I need to come back to it.
**Model:** 唔該，咁就清楚喇。我會留返確認紀錄，之後要跟進都有得查。

## D034 — Mental health care plan

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你之前要求我哋解釋心理輔導轉介。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。
**Model:** You asked us to explain a counselling referral. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. I can see that the GP appointment is next Wednesday, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.
**Model:** 係。我見到家庭醫生預約係下星期三，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 而家要睇嘅重點係心理健康治療計劃。一般最重要嘅一點係Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。
**Model:** The key expression is mental health treatment plan. The important general point is that Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I need to deal with it before the end of this month. Otherwise, I’m worried that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.
**Model:** 我需要喺今個月底之前處理。否則，我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我快快問多句：嗰份通知之後冇再改過？
**Model:** One quick check: nothing has changed since that notice?

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Correct; that’s the current amount on my side.
**Model:** 啱，我呢邊而家都仲係嗰個金額。

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。
**Model:** The safest next step is to ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have Medicare card and referral letters, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?
**Model:** 我有Medicare 卡同轉介信，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？
**Model:** Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have copies of most of them; one is still outstanding.
**Model:** 大部分都有副本，仲欠一份資料。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該。經診所病人網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the clinic patient portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** While they’re checking it, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?
**Model:** 佢哋核對緊嗰陣，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約七至十個工作日。期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助。
**Model:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about seven to ten business days. Meanwhile, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I’m still unhappy after I get the written decision, how do I challenge it through the right process?
**Model:** 如果收到書面決定之後我都仲唔滿意，我要點樣跟正式程序提出覆核？

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。
**Model:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.

### Segment 16 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of mental health treatment plan with the reference number for future contact.
**Model:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將心理健康治療計劃同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。

## D035 — Hospital discharge follow-up

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Let’s check the progress of hospital discharge instructions. All right, I’ve got the matter on screen. What do you need help with first?
**Model:** 我哋睇下出院跟進指示嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，好，我而家睇到件事嘅資料。你想我先幫你處理邊一部分？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 之前有人話跟進預約係七日後。我仍然擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
**Model:** Earlier I was told the follow-up appointment is in seven days. I’m still concerned that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** This comes down to discharge summary. Remember that Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.
**Model:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係出院摘要。要留意Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 有冇其他資料想我而家交，而唔係等過咗通知所列日期先補？
**Model:** Do you need anything else from me now, rather than after the date shown on the notice?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The next step is to ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
**Model:** 下一步係要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗出院摘要同藥物清單。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
**Model:** I have discharge summary and medication list. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Use the clinic patient portal, then keep the submission receipt. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
**Model:** 可以。用診所病人網上平台提交，之後保存確認編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我做完整個申請之後，我應該等幾耐先再打電話？
**Model:** When I’ve finished the application, how long should I leave it before I call?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about two weeks. During that period, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen, and check the official contact channel regularly.
**Model:** 預大約兩星期。期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果佢哋未處理完之前我搬屋或者換電話，我係咪更新返而家個申請，定係等有決定先？
**Model:** If I change address or phone number before they finish, do I update the current request or wait for the decision first?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
**Model:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低出院摘要，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
**Model:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down discharge summary so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D036 — Antenatal appointment

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 早晨。呢度係診所行政職員。我聯絡你係關於產前診所預約。我睇到你之前個查詢紀錄。你而家最擔心係邊一部分呀？
**Model:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a clinic administration officer. I’m calling about an antenatal clinic booking. I can see the notes from your enquiry. What’s the part you’re most concerned about?

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My concern is that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost. I’d like this resolved before the next scheduled appointment or payment.
**Model:** 我最擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。我想喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理好。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 而家要睇嘅重點係產前護理。Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。
**Model:** What we need to look at is antenatal care. Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. I’ll check the details on your case now.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My paperwork says the first hospital appointment is on 22 September. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?
**Model:** 我份文件寫住第一次醫院預約係九月二十二日。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。
**Model:** The record matches that detail. Next, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. Check every date and number before submitting.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have referral, Medicare card and scan reports. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗轉介信、Medicare 卡同超聲波報告。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用診所病人網上平台。上載轉介信、Medicare 卡同超聲波報告，之後保存收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the clinic patient portal. Upload referral, Medicare card and scan reports, then save the receipt or reference.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** How long should I wait? I’m worried that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?
**Model:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約三至四星期。期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。
**Model:** Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I still think the decision is wrong, is there a formal review process I can use?
**Model:** 如果我睇完都覺得個決定唔啱，係咪有正式嘅覆核程序可以跟？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。
**Model:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Fine, I’ll do it that way and keep proof of the date I sent it.
**Model:** 好，我會咁樣做，亦會留返提交日期嘅證明。

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔該。我會保存收據，同埋留返啲文件副本。
**Model:** Thanks. I’ll make sure I keep the receipt and a copy of the documents.

## D037 — Checking VEVO

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks for calling a migration service officer. I can see an enquiry about a visa-status check. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
**Model:** 多謝你打嚟移民服務職員。我見到有一個關於簽證狀況查核嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到網上簽證權益查核系統，即係 VEVO。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
**Model:** I received an update today. It mentions Visa Entitlement Verification Online, or VEVO, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That term matters because visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record. Your current record also says the passport was renewed last month.
**Model:** 呢個詞重要，因為簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。你目前紀錄亦顯示上個月換咗新護照。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下星期五之前知道，因為可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。
**Model:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before next Friday, because I may misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, check the current official notice and update the relevant details online.
**Model:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗護照同簽證批准通知。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
**Model:** I’ve gathered passport and visa grant notice. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Are you able to show me those records today?
**Model:** 你今日方唔方便畀我睇嗰啲紀錄？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 有，淨係仲有一份文件要遲啲先拎到。
**Model:** Yes. There’s just one document I need to bring later.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Use your ImmiAccount unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
**Model:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用你嘅 ImmiAccount。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我今日搞掂晒啲文件，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
**Model:** If I get the paperwork in today, what happens while the matter is being checked?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The usual guide is about five business days. While you wait, do not assume new work or travel rights until the current conditions are checked, and monitor any official messages.
**Model:** 一般參考時間係大約五個工作日。等候期間，未核實現有條件之前，唔好自行假設有新嘅工作或者旅行權利，並留意任何正式訊息。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果最後個決定對我不利，佢哋會唔會講明係申請邊一部分出問題？
**Model:** If the final answer goes against me, will they tell me what part of the application caused the problem?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.
**Model:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返網上簽證權益查核系統，即係 VEVO呢個詞。
**Model:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use Visa Entitlement Verification Online, or VEVO when I contact the service again.

## D038 — Bridging Visa A

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我係跟進過橋簽證狀況查詢。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。
**Model:** I’m following up on a bridging-visa status query. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that the current substantive visa expires on 30 September. I’m nervous that I might misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions.
**Model:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係現有實質簽證喺九月三十日到期。我擔心可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 決定下一步之前，要記住A 類過橋簽證呢個詞。喺呢類事情，簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。
**Model:** This comes down to Bridging Visa A. In this type of matter, visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What are the consequences if I can’t resolve this by the end of this month?
**Model:** 如果我喺今個月底之前處理唔到，實際會有咩後果？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。
**Model:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, check the current official notice and update the relevant details online. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Will visa grant notices and passport be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?
**Model:** 我有簽證批准通知同護照。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你而家手上有冇大部分嗰啲文件？
**Model:** Do you have most of that paperwork available?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Nearly all. I can get the missing one this evening.
**Model:** 差唔多齊，我今晚可以攞埋欠嗰份。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 先經你嘅 ImmiAccount提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。
**Model:** Start with the requested material through your ImmiAccount. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Will I get anything showing the upload went through, That date could matter if there’s a dispute later.
**Model:** 我上載完會唔會有嘢證明成功咗，如果之後有爭議，嗰個日期可能好重要㗎。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 保存網上確認資料。預大約七至十個工作日；期間，未核實現有條件之前，唔好自行假設有新嘅工作或者旅行權利。
**Model:** Note the lodgement reference. Allow about seven to ten business days; during that time, do not assume new work or travel rights until the current conditions are checked.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the due time passes without a response, what’s the right way to chase it up without duplicating the request?
**Model:** 如果應該有回覆嗰陣都冇聲氣，點樣跟進先唔會變咗重複申請？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。
**Model:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Bridging Visa A. That should help me explain the issue clearly.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住A 類過橋簽證。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。
**Model:** Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.

## D039 — Bridging Visa B travel

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** You asked us to explain proposed overseas travel. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
**Model:** 你之前要求我哋解釋計劃出境旅行。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係。我見到計劃十月份出境十日，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
**Model:** Yes. I can see that travel is planned for ten days in October, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The key expression is Bridging Visa B. The important general point is that visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record.
**Model:** 呢件事最緊要先搞清楚B 類過橋簽證。一般最重要嘅一點係簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。我幫你睇吓你個情況實際點樣套用。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我需要喺通知所列日期之前處理。否則，我擔心可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。
**Model:** I need to deal with it before the date shown on the notice. Otherwise, What worries me is that I could misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Just checking: that’s still the latest version you received?
**Model:** 確認一下，呢個係你最近收到嗰個版本？

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 啱，我今朝都再對過一次。
**Model:** Correct. I checked it again this morning.

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The safest next step is to check the current official notice and update the relevant details online. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.
**Model:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有旅行日期、護照同簽證通知，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
**Model:** I have travel dates, passport and visa notice, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Have you brought the records we just discussed?
**Model:** 頭先講嗰啲紀錄你有冇帶嚟？

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 差唔多全部有，不過仲有一樣冇帶喺身。
**Model:** I’ve got nearly everything, but one item isn’t with me.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Upload the evidence through your ImmiAccount and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
**Model:** 應該。經你嘅 ImmiAccount上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 佢哋核對緊嗰陣，會唔會有額外收費或者其他後果㗎？
**Model:** While they’re checking it, could I be charged anything extra or face another consequence?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, do not assume new work or travel rights until the current conditions are checked.
**Model:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約兩星期。期間，未核實現有條件之前，唔好自行假設有新嘅工作或者旅行權利。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果收到書面決定之後我都仲唔滿意，我下一步應該搵邊個，先唔使又打同一輪電話？
**Model:** If I’m still unhappy after I get the written decision, who should I contact next instead of making the same phone call again?

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.
**Model:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。

### Segment 16 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將B 類過橋簽證同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
**Model:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of Bridging Visa B with the reference number for future contact.

## D040 — Visa condition enquiry

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我哋睇下現有簽證條件嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。
**Model:** Let’s check the progress of current visa conditions. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Earlier I was told a new roster would add twelve work hours a week. I’m still concerned that I may misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions.
**Model:** 之前有人話新更表每星期會多十二個工作鐘。我仍然擔心可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係簽證條件。要留意簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。
**Model:** I can see that note. The key term on this file is visa condition. Remember that visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Do you need anything else from me now, rather than after the next scheduled appointment or payment?
**Model:** 有冇其他資料想我而家交，而唔係等過咗下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期先補？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 下一步係查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。
**Model:** The next step is to check the current official notice and update the relevant details online. The service can then check whether anything further is required.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have visa grant notice and proposed roster. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗簽證批准通知同建議更表。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。用你嘅 ImmiAccount提交，之後保存網上確認資料。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。
**Model:** Yes. Use your ImmiAccount, then keep the submission receipt. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Once the documents are in, what’s a sensible time to ask for an update?
**Model:** 啲文件交齊之後，大概幾時再問吓會比較合理？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約三至四星期。期間，未核實現有條件之前，唔好自行假設有新嘅工作或者旅行權利，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。
**Model:** Allow about three to four weeks. During that period, do not assume new work or travel rights until the current conditions are checked, and check the official contact channel regularly.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If there’s a material change before the matter is finalised, should I tell them straight away?
**Model:** 如果件事完成之前有重要變更，我係咪應該即刻通知佢哋？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。
**Model:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down visa condition so I can refer to the matter accurately.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低簽證條件，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。

## D041 — Updating passport details

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a migration service officer. I’m calling about new passport details. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.
**Model:** 早晨。呢度係移民服務職員。我聯絡你係關於新護照資料。多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有啲驚會誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。我想喺下星期五之前處理好。
**Model:** I’m concerned I might misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions. I’d like this resolved before next Friday.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The part that matters here is ImmiAccount update. visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
**Model:** 而家要睇嘅重點係ImmiAccount 資料更新。簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我份文件寫住新護照喺八月二日簽發。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
**Model:** My paperwork says the new passport was issued on 2 August. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The record matches that detail. Next, check the current official notice and update the relevant details online. Check every date and number before submitting.
**Model:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗新護照個人資料頁。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
**Model:** I have new passport biodata page. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use your ImmiAccount. Upload new passport biodata page, then save the receipt or reference.
**Model:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用你嘅 ImmiAccount。上載新護照個人資料頁，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
**Model:** How long should I wait? My worry is that I might misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about five business days. Meanwhile, do not assume new work or travel rights until the current conditions are checked. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
**Model:** 預大約五個工作日。期間，未核實現有條件之前，唔好自行假設有新嘅工作或者旅行權利。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果個結果我點睇都覺得唔對路，我應該先攞書面理由，定係即刻申請覆核？
**Model:** If the result still looks wrong to me, should I ask for written reasons first, or request a review straight away?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.
**Model:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 明白。我會留返確認紀錄，有需要先再跟進。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the confirmation and follow up only if I need to.

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thank you. I’ll check the details once more before I submit anything.
**Model:** 唔該。我提交之前會再對一次啲資料。

## D042 — Citizenship appointment

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝你打嚟移民服務職員。我見到有一個關於入籍預約嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。
**Model:** Thanks for calling a migration service officer. I can see an enquiry about a citizenship appointment. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I received an update today. It mentions identity documents, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.
**Model:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到身份證明文件。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個詞重要，因為簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。你目前紀錄亦顯示預約係十月十五日上午九點半。
**Model:** That term matters because visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record. Your current record also says the appointment is at 9:30 am on 15 October.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the end of this month, because I may misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions.
**Model:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺今個月底之前知道，因為可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。
**Model:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, check the current official notice and update the relevant details online.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve gathered passport, photo ID and appointment letter. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗護照、有相身份證同預約信。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？
**Model:** Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have the main records. One document still needs to be downloaded.
**Model:** 主要紀錄有齊，仲有一份文件要下載返。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用你嘅 ImmiAccount。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。
**Model:** Use your ImmiAccount unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Say I upload all of this this afternoon, what happens while the matter is being checked?
**Model:** 假設我今日下晝上載晒，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 一般參考時間係大約七至十個工作日。等候期間，未核實現有條件之前，唔好自行假設有新嘅工作或者旅行權利，並留意任何正式訊息。
**Model:** The usual guide is about seven to ten business days. While you wait, do not assume new work or travel rights until the current conditions are checked, and monitor any official messages.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If they eventually say no, can I see the reasons before deciding whether to seek a review?
**Model:** 如果佢哋最後話唔得，我可唔可以睇咗理由先，再決定使唔使申請覆核？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。
**Model:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use identity documents when I contact the service again.
**Model:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返身份證明文件呢個詞。

## D043 — AMEP English classes

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’m following up on English-class options after settlement. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
**Model:** 我係跟進定居後英文課程選擇。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係星期二同星期四夜晚有時間。我擔心可能錯過註冊程序或者合適課程選擇。
**Model:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that Tuesday and Thursday evenings are available. I’m concerned I might miss an enrolment step or a suitable class option.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Before deciding the next step, remember the term Adult Migrant English Program, or AMEP. In this type of matter, AMEP eligibility and class options depend on individual eligibility and available providers.
**Model:** 決定下一步之前，要記住成人移民英語計劃，即係 AMEP呢個詞。喺呢類事情，AMEP 資格同上課選擇會視乎個人資格同可用課程提供者。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 去到通知所列日期件事仲未解決，我應該預咩情況？
**Model:** If the date shown on the notice arrives and this is still unresolved, what should I expect?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, ask for an AMEP eligibility assessment and suitable class options. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
**Model:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，查詢 AMEP 資格評估同合適上課選擇。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有資格資料同有相身份證。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
**Model:** Will eligibility details and photo ID be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Are those documents available on your phone now?
**Model:** 嗰啲文件而家用電話開唔開到？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 大部分有呀，不過仲有一份喺屋企。
**Model:** I have most of them; one is still at home.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Start with the requested material through the service enrolment portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
**Model:** 先經服務註冊網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我點樣先知佢哋真係收到啲文件，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。
**Model:** How can I tell they’ve actually received the documents, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Note the lodgement reference. Allow about two weeks; during that time, keep your identity and eligibility documents ready for enrolment.
**Model:** 保存確認編號。預大約兩星期；期間，準備好身份同資格文件，方便註冊。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 假如到時我仲係等緊，點樣跟進先唔會變咗重複申請？
**Model:** Suppose I’m still waiting after that, what’s the right way to chase it up without duplicating the request?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask the provider or settlement service to explain the available enrolment options.
**Model:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，要求課程提供者或者定居服務解釋可用註冊選擇。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住成人移民英語計劃，即係 AMEP。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Adult Migrant English Program, or AMEP. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. I’ll make sure I keep the receipt and a copy of the documents.
**Model:** 唔該。我會保存收據，同埋留返啲文件副本。

## D044 — Qualification recognition

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你之前要求我哋解釋海外學歷查詢。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。
**Model:** You asked us to explain an overseas qualification enquiry. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. I can see that the degree was completed in Hong Kong in 2022, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.
**Model:** 係。我見到二零二二年喺香港完成學位，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個情況主要關乎學歷評估。一般最重要嘅一點係評估途徑會視乎學歷、用途同相關評估機構。不如我哋先核對你而家嘅資料，再決定下一步。
**Model:** The key expression is qualification assessment. The important general point is that the assessment pathway depends on the qualification, purpose and relevant assessing authority.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I need to deal with it before I submit my next employment or study application. Otherwise, I’m anxious that I may follow the wrong recognition pathway for work or study.
**Model:** 我需要喺我提交下一份工作或者升學申請之前處理。否則，我擔心可能用錯工作或者升學所需嘅學歷認可途徑。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 即係冇另一個更新嘅數字要睇？
**Model:** So there isn’t a newer figure I should be looking at?

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, I’m still working from that figure.
**Model:** 啱呀，我而家都仲係按呢個數字。

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係搵出相關評估機構並提交所需學歷文件。資料要按事實填寫，亦要確保姓名同日期同你嘅紀錄一致。
**Model:** The safest next step is to identify the relevant assessing authority and submit the required qualification documents. Keep the information factual and make sure names and dates match your records.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have a degree certificate and academic transcript, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?
**Model:** 我有學位證書同成績表，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 啲證明文件而家有冇喺身？
**Model:** Are those supporting papers with you now?

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have most of them; one is still at home.
**Model:** 大部分有呀，不過仲有一份喺屋企。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該。經相關評估機構嘅網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the relevant assessing body’s online portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Until they make a decision, could I be charged anything extra or face another consequence?
**Model:** 等佢哋作決定之前，會唔會有額外收費或者其他後果㗎？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約三至四星期。期間，準備好學歷文件正本同任何所需翻譯。
**Model:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, keep original qualification documents and any required translations available.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I’m still unhappy after I get the written decision, who should I contact next instead of making the same phone call again?
**Model:** 如果收到書面決定之後我都仲唔滿意，我下一步應該搵邊個，先唔使又打同一輪電話？

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，要求評估機構解釋評估或者覆核程序。
**Model:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask the assessing body to explain its assessment or review process.

### Segment 16 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of qualification assessment with the reference number for future contact.
**Model:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將學歷評估同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。

## D045 — Traffic fine court option

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Let’s check the progress of a traffic infringement notice. I’ve found the enquiry. Before we get into the details, tell me what you need resolved.
**Model:** 我哋睇下交通違規罰款通知嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我搵到你個查詢喇。講細節之前，你最想解決嘅係咩呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 之前有人話通知要求九月九日前處理。我仍然擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。
**Model:** Earlier I was told the notice requires action by 9 September. I’m still concerned that I may miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** For this matter, we need to be clear about infringement notice. Remember that deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. Let me check how that applies to you.
**Model:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係違規罰款通知。要留意除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我呢邊而家係咪齊晒？我唔想過咗下星期五先知有嘢漏咗。
**Model:** Is my side complete now? I don’t want to discover something is missing after next Friday.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The next step is to get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
**Model:** 下一步係盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗通知、駕駛執照資料同相片。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
**Model:** I have notice, licence details and photographs. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Use the court or service online portal, then note the lodgement reference. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
**Model:** 可以。用法院或者服務機構網上平台提交，之後留返提交收據。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 上載完成之後，大概幾時再問吓會比較合理？
**Model:** After the upload is complete, what’s a sensible time to ask for an update?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about five business days. During that period, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction, and check the official contact channel regularly.
**Model:** 預大約五個工作日。期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我等緊期間有重要資料改咗，點樣更新會最穩陣？
**Model:** What if something important changes while I’m waiting, what’s the safest way to update it?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
**Model:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低違規罰款通知，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
**Model:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down infringement notice so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D046 — NSW AVO enquiry

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 早晨。呢度係社區法律服務職員。我聯絡你係關於新州 AVO 個案。我睇到你之前個查詢紀錄。你而家最擔心係邊一部分呀？
**Model:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a community legal service officer. I’m calling about an AVO matter in New South Wales. I can see the notes from your enquiry. What’s the part you’re most concerned about?

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’m nervous that I might miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction. I’d like this resolved before the end of this month.
**Model:** 我就係怕可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。我想喺今個月底之前處理好。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個情況主要關乎暴力禁制令，即係 AVO。除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。不如我哋先核對你而家嘅資料，再決定下一步。
**Model:** What we need to look at is Apprehended Violence Order, or AVO. deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. I’ll check the details on your case now.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My paperwork says the next Local Court date is 21 September. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?
**Model:** 我份文件寫住下一次地方法院日期係九月二十一日。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。
**Model:** The record matches that detail. Next, get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. Check every date and number before submitting.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have court papers and interim order. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗法院文件同臨時命令。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用法院或者服務機構網上平台。上載法院文件同臨時命令，之後保存收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the court or service online portal. Upload court papers and interim order, then save the receipt or reference.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** How long should I wait? I’m concerned I might miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?
**Model:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約七至十個工作日。期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。
**Model:** Allow about seven to ten business days. Meanwhile, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I still think the decision is wrong, should I ask for written reasons first, or request a review straight away?
**Model:** 如果我睇完都覺得個決定唔啱，我應該先攞書面理由，定係即刻申請覆核？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。
**Model:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks for your help. I’ll follow those steps and check the next notice carefully.
**Model:** 唔該晒。我會照住啲步驟做，之後收到新通知亦會睇清楚。

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 明白。我會留返確認紀錄，有需要先再跟進。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the confirmation and follow up only if I need to.

## D047 — Bail reporting condition

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks for calling a community legal service officer. I can see an enquiry about bail reporting conditions. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
**Model:** 多謝你打嚟社區法律服務職員。我見到有一個關於保釋報到條件嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到保釋條件。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
**Model:** I received an update today. It mentions bail condition, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That term matters because deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. Your current record also says reporting is required every Monday before 6 pm.
**Model:** 呢個詞重要，因為除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。你目前紀錄亦顯示逢星期一下午六點前要報到。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺通知所列日期之前知道，因為可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。
**Model:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the date shown on the notice, because I may miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process.
**Model:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗保釋文件同工作更表。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
**Model:** I’ve gathered bail undertaking and work roster. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Can you pull up those documents on your phone?
**Model:** 你可唔可以而家用電話開返啲文件？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 大部分帶咗，不過有一頁留咗喺屋企。
**Model:** I brought most of it, but one page is at home.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Use the court or service online portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
**Model:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用法院或者服務機構網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 假設我今日下晝上載晒，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
**Model:** Say I upload all of this this afternoon, what happens while the matter is being checked?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The usual guide is about two weeks. While you wait, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction, and monitor any official messages.
**Model:** 一般參考時間係大約兩星期。等候期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示，並留意任何正式訊息。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我收到一個負面決定，我可唔可以叫佢哋講清楚點解，再決定係咪覆核？
**Model:** If I get a negative decision, can I ask them to explain exactly why before I challenge it?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
**Model:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返保釋條件呢個詞。
**Model:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use bail condition when I contact the service again.

## D048 — Witness statement

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我係跟進錄取證人供詞預約。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。
**Model:** I’m following up on a witness-statement appointment. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that the interview is booked for Thursday afternoon. I’m concerned I might miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.
**Model:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係會面安排喺星期四下晝。我擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 決定下一步之前，要記住證人供詞呢個詞。喺呢類事情，除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。
**Model:** What we need to look at is witness statement. In this type of matter, deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. I’ll check the details on your case now.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the next scheduled appointment or payment arrives and this is still unresolved, what should I expect?
**Model:** 去到下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期件事仲未解決，我應該預咩情況？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。
**Model:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Will appointment letter and relevant messages be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?
**Model:** 我有預約信同相關訊息。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 頭先提過嗰啲證明你而家開唔開到？
**Model:** Can you access the evidence we just mentioned?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I can show you the main documents; one record is missing.
**Model:** 主要文件我可以畀你睇，仲有一份紀錄未搵到。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 先經法院或者服務機構網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。
**Model:** Start with the requested material through the court or service online portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Will I get anything showing the upload went through, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.
**Model:** 我上載完會唔會有嘢證明成功咗，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 記低提交參考編號。預大約三至四星期；期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示。
**Model:** Save the online confirmation. Allow about three to four weeks; during that time, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Suppose I’m still waiting after that, should I follow up the existing request or submit another one?
**Model:** 假如到時我仲係等緊，我應該跟返原本個申請，定係再交一份？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。
**Model:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember witness statement. That should help me explain the issue clearly.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住證人供詞。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好極喇。我會處理好，亦會留返確認紀錄。
**Model:** Perfect. I’ll sort it out and keep the confirmation for later.

## D049 — Family dispute mediation

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** You asked us to explain a family dispute-resolution appointment. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
**Model:** 你之前要求我哋解釋家庭爭議解決預約。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係。我見到調解安排喺十月六日，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
**Model:** Yes. I can see that the mediation is scheduled for 6 October, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The key expression is family dispute resolution. The important general point is that deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed.
**Model:** 我哋而家要睇家庭爭議解決。一般最重要嘅一點係除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。我即刻幫你對一對件紀錄。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我需要喺下星期五之前處理。否則，我擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。
**Model:** I need to deal with it before next Friday. Otherwise, I’m concerned I might miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I want to make sure that date is the current one.
**Model:** 我想確定嗰個日期係而家最新嗰個。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我收到最新嘅紀錄就係呢份。
**Model:** That’s the newest record I’ve received.

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The safest next step is to get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.
**Model:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有預約信同育兒安排建議，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
**Model:** I have appointment letter and parenting proposals, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Did you bring the paperwork we talked about?
**Model:** 頭先講嗰啲文件你有冇帶埋嚟？

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 主要紀錄有齊，仲有一份文件要下載返。
**Model:** I have the main records. One document still needs to be downloaded.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the court or service online portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
**Model:** 應該。經法院或者服務機構網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 佢哋未核對完份紀錄之前，會唔會有額外收費或者其他後果㗎？
**Model:** Before they finish reviewing the record, could I be charged anything extra or face another consequence?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about five business days. Meanwhile, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction.
**Model:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約五個工作日。期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我睇完書面決定都仲係唔明，正確下一步係咩？我唔想不停打去唔同地方。
**Model:** If the written decision still doesn’t make sense, what’s the proper next step? I don’t want to keep ringing different people.

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
**Model:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

### Segment 16 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將家庭爭議解決同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
**Model:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of family dispute resolution with the reference number for future contact.

## D050 — Small civil claim

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我哋睇下小額民事索償嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我睇到你之前個查詢紀錄。你而家最擔心係邊一部分呀？
**Model:** Let’s check the progress of a small civil claim. I can see the notes from your enquiry. What’s the part you’re most concerned about?

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Earlier I was told the amount in dispute is $3,200. I’m still concerned that I may miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.
**Model:** 之前有人話爭議金額係三千二百澳元。我仍然擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係民事索償。要留意除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。
**Model:** I can see that note. The key term on this file is civil claim. Remember that deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Could you check the file once more before the end of this month, just in case I’ve left something out?
**Model:** 今個月底之前可唔可以再幫我對一次份紀錄，睇吓有冇漏？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 下一步係盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。
**Model:** The next step is to get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. The service can then check whether anything further is required.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have bank transfers and written messages. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗銀行轉賬紀錄同書面訊息。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。用法院或者服務機構網上平台提交，之後保存網上確認資料。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。
**Model:** Yes. Use the court or service online portal, then save the confirmation number. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** After I submit it, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?
**Model:** 我交咗之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約七至十個工作日。期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。
**Model:** Allow about seven to ten business days. During that period, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction, and check the official contact channel regularly.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Suppose one of my details changes during the assessment, how do I make sure the new information is attached to this matter?
**Model:** 假如評估期間我有一項資料改咗，點樣先可以確保新資料加返落同一件事度？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。
**Model:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down civil claim so I can refer to the matter accurately.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低民事索償，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。

## D051 — Legal aid eligibility

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a community legal service officer. I’m calling about a legal-aid enquiry. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
**Model:** 早晨。呢度係社區法律服務職員。我聯絡你係關於法律援助查詢。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我就係怕可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。我想喺通知所列日期之前處理好。
**Model:** What worries me is that I may miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction. I’d like this resolved before the date shown on the notice.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The part that matters here is legal aid. deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
**Model:** 而家要睇嘅重點係法律援助。除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我份文件寫住三個星期後要上庭。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
**Model:** My paperwork says the court date is in three weeks. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The record matches that detail. Next, get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. Check every date and number before submitting.
**Model:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗收入證明同法院文件。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
**Model:** I have income evidence and court documents. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the court or service online portal. Upload income evidence and court documents, then save the receipt or reference.
**Model:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用法院或者服務機構網上平台。上載收入證明同法院文件，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
**Model:** How long should I wait? My worry is that I might miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
**Model:** 預大約兩星期。期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 假如我覺得個決定有問題，我可唔可以先要求覆核，再決定使唔使投訴呀？
**Model:** Suppose the decision doesn’t seem right, can I ask for a review before making a complaint?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
**Model:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔該。我提交之前會再對一次啲資料。
**Model:** Thank you. I’ll check the details once more before I submit anything.

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** All right, I know what to do now. I’ll keep a copy of whatever I send.
**Model:** 好，而家我知點做喇。我交出去嘅資料都會留返副本。

## D052 — Requesting an adjournment

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝你打嚟社區法律服務職員。我見到有一個關於即將進行嘅法院聆訊嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。
**Model:** Thanks for calling a community legal service officer. I can see an enquiry about an upcoming court hearing. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I received an update today. It mentions adjournment, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.
**Model:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到押後聆訊。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個詞重要，因為除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。你目前紀錄亦顯示聆訊排期係九月三日。
**Model:** That term matters because deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. Your current record also says the hearing is listed for 3 September.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the next scheduled appointment or payment, because I may miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.
**Model:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前知道，因為可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。
**Model:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve gathered court notice and medical evidence. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗法院通知同醫療證明。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你可唔可以而家用電話開返啲文件？
**Model:** Can you pull up those documents on your phone?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have the main ones. One file is on my home computer.
**Model:** 主要嗰幾份有，一個檔案仲喺屋企部電腦。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用法院或者服務機構網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。
**Model:** Use the court or service online portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I send everything today, what happens while the matter is being checked?
**Model:** 如果我今日交齊啲資料，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 一般參考時間係大約三至四星期。等候期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示，並留意任何正式訊息。
**Model:** The usual guide is about three to four weeks. While you wait, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction, and monitor any official messages.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the application is refused, can I ask them to explain exactly why before I challenge it?
**Model:** 如果個申請畀人拒絕，我可唔可以叫佢哋講清楚點解，再決定係咪覆核？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。
**Model:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use adjournment when I contact the service again.
**Model:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返押後聆訊呢個詞。

## D053 — Hard-waste collection

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’m following up on a council hard-waste booking. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
**Model:** 我係跟進市議會大型廢物收集預約。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係預約收集日期係九月十四日。我擔心可能錯過預約或者用唔到服務。
**Model:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that the booking is for 14 September. What worries me is that I may miss the booking or lose access to the service.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What we need to look at is hard-waste collection. In this type of matter, local services usually depend on the correct address, eligibility and booking details. I’ll check the details on your case now.
**Model:** 決定下一步之前，要記住大型廢物收集呢個詞。喺呢類事情，本地服務通常視乎正確地址、資格同預約資料。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我喺下星期五之前處理唔到，實際會有咩後果？
**Model:** What are the consequences if I can’t resolve this by next Friday?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, submit the council booking or application with the requested information. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
**Model:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，連同所需資料提交市議會預約或者申請。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有預約確認同物業地址。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
**Model:** Will booking confirmation and property address be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Do you have the supporting documents with you today?
**Model:** 你今日有冇帶埋啲證明文件？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 差唔多齊晒，我返屋企可以上載最後一份。
