**Model:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of urgent repair with the reference number for future contact.

## D080 — Rent increase notice

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我哋睇下加租通知嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？
**Model:** Let’s check the progress of a rent increase notice. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Earlier I was told rent may rise from $540 to $610 a week on 20 October. I’m still concerned that I may pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.
**Model:** 之前有人話十月二十日起週租可能由五百四十加到六百一十澳元。我仍然擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係加租通知。要留意租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。
**Model:** What we need to look at is rent increase notice. Remember that tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. I’ll check the details on your case now.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Could you check the file once more before the next scheduled appointment or payment, just in case I’ve left something out?
**Model:** 下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前可唔可以再幫我對一次份紀錄，睇吓有冇漏？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 下一步係書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。
**Model:** The next step is to write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence. The service can then check whether anything further is required.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have tenancy agreement and increase notice. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗租約同加租通知。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。用租務網上平台提交，之後保存確認編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。
**Model:** Yes. Use the tenancy online portal, then note the lodgement reference. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** After I submit it, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?
**Model:** 我交咗之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約三至四星期。期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。
**Model:** Allow about three to four weeks. During that period, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records, and check the official contact channel regularly.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If there’s a material change before the matter is finalised, what’s the safest way to update it?
**Model:** 如果件事完成之前有重要變更，點樣更新會最穩陣？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。
**Model:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down rent increase notice so I can refer to the matter accurately.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低加租通知，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。

## D081 — Ending a tenancy

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a property management officer. I’m calling about a notice to end a tenancy. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
**Model:** 早晨。呢度係物業管理職員。我聯絡你係關於終止租約通知。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 而家我最憂心係可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。我想喺下星期五之前處理好。
**Model:** I’m concerned I might pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline. I’d like this resolved before next Friday.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The part that matters here is termination notice. tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
**Model:** 我哋而家要睇終止租約通知。租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。我即刻幫你對一對件紀錄。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我份文件寫住預計搬走日期係九月三十日。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
**Model:** My paperwork says the proposed move-out date is 30 September. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The record matches that detail. Next, write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence. Check every date and number before submitting.
**Model:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗租約同準備發出嘅書面通知。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
**Model:** I have lease and proposed written notice. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the tenancy online portal. Upload lease and proposed written notice, then save the receipt or reference.
**Model:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用租務網上平台。上載租約同準備發出嘅書面通知，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
**Model:** How long should I wait? I’m concerned I might pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about five business days. Meanwhile, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
**Model:** 預大約五個工作日。期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果個結果我點睇都覺得唔對路，我應該搵邊個再睇一次個決定？
**Model:** If the result still looks wrong to me, who do I ask to look at the decision again?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
**Model:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 明白。我會留返確認紀錄，有需要先再跟進。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the confirmation and follow up only if I need to.

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good, I’ll do that. If anything changes, I’ll let the service know straight away.
**Model:** 好呀，我會照做。如果有任何改動，我會即刻通知返個機構。

## D082 — Breaking a fixed-term lease

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝你打嚟物業管理職員。我見到有一個關於提早離開定期租約嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。
**Model:** Thanks for calling a property management officer. I can see an enquiry about leaving a fixed-term lease early. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I received an update today. It mentions break lease, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.
**Model:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到提前終止租約。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個詞重要，因為租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。你目前紀錄亦顯示定期租約仲有四個月。
**Model:** That term matters because tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. Your current record also says four months remain on the fixed term.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the end of this month, because I may pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.
**Model:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺今個月底之前知道，因為可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。
**Model:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve gathered lease and proposed move-out date. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗租約同預計搬走日期。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 頭先講嗰啲文件你有冇帶埋嚟？
**Model:** Did you bring the paperwork we talked about?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, apart from one record I still need to find.
**Model:** 有呀，不過仲有一份紀錄我要再搵吓。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用租務網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。
**Model:** Use the tenancy online portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Say I upload all of this this afternoon, is there anything I should or shouldn’t do while I’m waiting?
**Model:** 假設我今日下晝上載晒，等緊期間有冇啲嘢一定要做或者唔好做呀？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 一般參考時間係大約七至十個工作日。等候期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄，並留意任何正式訊息。
**Model:** The usual guide is about seven to ten business days. While you wait, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records, and monitor any official messages.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I get a negative decision, can I ask them to explain exactly why before I challenge it?
**Model:** 如果我收到一個負面決定，我可唔可以叫佢哋講清楚點解，再決定係咪覆核？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。
**Model:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use break lease when I contact the service again.
**Model:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返提前終止租約呢個詞。

## D083 — Bond refund dispute

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’m following up on a bond refund dispute. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
**Model:** 我係跟進按金退款爭議。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係建議清潔扣款係四百二十澳元。我擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。
**Model:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that the proposed cleaning deduction is $420. I’m nervous that I might pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** For this matter, we need to be clear about bond claim. In this type of matter, tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. Let me check how that applies to you.
**Model:** 決定下一步之前，要記住按金申索呢個詞。喺呢類事情，租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果通知所列日期之前都搞唔掂，問題會有幾大呀？
**Model:** How serious is it if I’m not able to fix this before the date shown on the notice?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
**Model:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有狀況報告、相片同清潔收據。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
**Model:** Will condition reports, photos and cleaning receipt be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Are the supporting documents handy at the moment?
**Model:** 啲證明文件而家喺唔喺手邊？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 大部分都有副本，仲欠一份資料。
**Model:** I have copies of most of them; one is still outstanding.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Start with the requested material through the tenancy online portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
**Model:** 先經租務網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我正式提交之後，點知系統已經有紀錄，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。
**Model:** Once I lodge it, how will I know it’s on their system, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Keep the submission receipt. Allow about two weeks; during that time, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records.
**Model:** 保存網上確認資料。預大約兩星期；期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果過咗嗰段時間都冇變化，我應該跟返原本個申請，定係再交一份？
**Model:** What if that timeframe passes and nothing changes, should I follow up the existing request or submit another one?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
**Model:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住按金申索。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember bond claim. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

## D084 — Routine inspection

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你之前要求我哋解釋例行租屋檢查。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。
**Model:** You asked us to explain a routine rental inspection. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. I can see that the inspection is booked for Tuesday at 10 am, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.
**Model:** 係。我見到檢查安排喺星期二朝早十點，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個情況主要關乎入屋通知。一般最重要嘅一點係租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。不如我哋先核對你而家嘅資料，再決定下一步。
**Model:** The key expression is entry notice. The important general point is that tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I need to deal with it before the next scheduled appointment or payment. Otherwise, I’m a bit worried I’ll pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.
**Model:** 我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理。否則，我擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 咁我可以按你頭先講嗰份資料去處理？
**Model:** Can I treat what you just told me as the current information?

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That’s still the current information.
**Model:** 呢個而家仲係最新資料。

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。
**Model:** The safest next step is to write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have inspection notice and work roster, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?
**Model:** 我有檢查通知同工作更表，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 頭先講嗰啲文件你有冇帶埋嚟？
**Model:** Did you bring the paperwork we talked about?

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have the main ones. One file is on my home computer.
**Model:** 主要嗰幾份有，一個檔案仲喺屋企部電腦。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該。經租務網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the tenancy online portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** While this is still being sorted out, could I be charged anything extra or face another consequence?
**Model:** 件事仲處理緊嗰陣，會唔會有額外收費或者其他後果㗎？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約三至四星期。期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄。
**Model:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the reasons don’t match what actually happened, how do I challenge it through the right process?
**Model:** 如果啲理由同實際發生嘅事對唔上，我要點樣跟正式程序提出覆核？

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。
**Model:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.

### Segment 16 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of entry notice with the reference number for future contact.
**Model:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將入屋通知同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。

## D085 — Shared utility bill

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Let’s check the progress of a shared electricity charge. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
**Model:** 我哋睇下分攤電費嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 之前有人話要求分攤二百三十澳元。我仍然擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。
**Model:** Earlier I was told the requested contribution is $230. I’m still concerned that I may pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The part that matters here is utility apportionment. Remember that tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
**Model:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係公用事業費用分攤。要留意租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 下星期五之前可唔可以再幫我對一次份紀錄，睇吓有冇漏？
**Model:** Could you check the file once more before next Friday, just in case I’ve left something out?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The next step is to write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
**Model:** 下一步係書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗賬單副本同租約。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
**Model:** I have bill copy and tenancy agreement. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Use the tenancy online portal, then note the lodgement reference. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
**Model:** 可以。用租務網上平台提交，之後留返提交收據。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我交咗之後，我應該等幾耐先再打電話？
**Model:** After I submit it, how long should I leave it before I call?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about five business days. During that period, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records, and check the official contact channel regularly.
**Model:** 預大約五個工作日。期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我等緊期間有重要資料改咗，我係咪應該即刻通知佢哋？
**Model:** What if something important changes while I’m waiting, should I tell them straight away?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
**Model:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低公用事業費用分攤，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
**Model:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down utility apportionment so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D086 — NSW tenancy tribunal

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 早晨。呢度係新南威爾士州租務資訊職員。我聯絡你係關於新州租務爭議。繼續之前，請確認你嘅全名同參考編號。
**Model:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a New South Wales tenancy information officer. I’m calling about a New South Wales tenancy dispute. Please confirm your full name and reference number before we continue.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My main worry is that I could pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline. I’d like this resolved before the end of this month.
**Model:** 我就係怕可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。我想喺今個月底之前處理好。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 關鍵用語係新州民事及行政審裁處，即係 NCAT。一般嚟講，合適嘅新州租務爭議途徑會視乎問題、證據同程序要求。我哋要核實你嘅實際資料。
**Model:** The key term is NSW Civil and Administrative Tribunal, or NCAT. Generally, the appropriate NSW tenancy dispute pathway depends on the issue, evidence and procedural requirements. We must check your exact facts.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My paperwork says the first repair request was sent six weeks ago. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?
**Model:** 我份文件寫住第一次維修要求係六星期前發出。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係整理租務證據並核實合適嘅新州爭議處理途徑。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。
**Model:** The record matches that detail. Next, organise the tenancy evidence and check the appropriate NSW dispute pathway. Check every date and number before submitting.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have lease, repair requests and photos. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗租約、維修要求同相片。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用相關新州租務或者審裁網上服務。上載租約、維修要求同相片，之後保存收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the relevant NSW tenancy or tribunal online service. Upload lease, repair requests and photos, then save the receipt or reference.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** How long should I wait? What worries me is that I could pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?
**Model:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約七至十個工作日。期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留所有維修通訊。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。
**Model:** Allow about seven to ten business days. Meanwhile, keep paying undisputed rent and keep all repair correspondence. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Suppose the decision doesn’t seem right, can I ask for a review before making a complaint?
**Model:** 假如我覺得個決定有問題，我可唔可以先要求覆核，再決定使唔使投訴呀？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，向相關租務服務查詢下一步，包括適用時嘅 NCAT 程序。
**Model:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask the relevant tenancy service about the next step, including NCAT where appropriate.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.
**Model:** 好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔該你解釋。我會等更新，同時將啲紀錄整理好。
**Model:** Thanks for explaining it. I’ll wait for the update and keep the records together.

## D087 — Motor claim excess

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks for calling an insurance claims officer. I can see an enquiry about a motor-insurance claim. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
**Model:** 多謝你打嚟保險索償主任。我見到有一個關於汽車保險索償嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到保單自付額。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
**Model:** I received an update today. It mentions policy excess, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That term matters because cover depends on the policy wording, limits, exclusions and any applicable excess. Your current record also says the quoted excess is $850.
**Model:** 呢個詞重要，因為保障視乎保單條款、上限、不保事項同適用自付額。你目前紀錄亦顯示所列自付額係八百五十澳元。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺通知所列日期之前知道，因為索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。
**Model:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the date shown on the notice, because the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, provide the available evidence and ask the insurer to review the claim.
**Model:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要提供現有證據，並要求保險公司覆核索償。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗保單資料表同索償通知。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
**Model:** I’ve gathered policy schedule and claim notice. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?
**Model:** 相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 有呀，不過今朝有一樣點都搵唔到。
**Model:** Yes, except for one item I couldn’t find this morning.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Use the insurer claims portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
**Model:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用保險公司索償網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我交咗啲文件之後，我係咪淨係等就得，定係仲有啲嘢要繼續做？
**Model:** Once I’ve submitted the documents, do I just wait, or is there something I need to keep doing?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The usual guide is about two weeks. While you wait, take reasonable steps to prevent further loss and keep evidence, and monitor any official messages.
**Model:** 一般參考時間係大約兩星期。等候期間，採取合理措施防止損失擴大，並保留證據，並留意任何正式訊息。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果佢哋最後話唔得，佢哋會唔會講明係申請邊一部分出問題？
**Model:** If they eventually say no, will they tell me what part of the application caused the problem?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, use internal dispute resolution, then AFCA if the complaint is eligible.
**Model:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，先用內部爭議處理程序，如投訴合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返保單自付額呢個詞。
**Model:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use policy excess when I contact the service again.

## D088 — Storm damage at home

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我係跟進暴風雨後家居保險索償。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。
**Model:** I’m following up on a home-insurance claim after a storm. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that the storm occurred on Saturday night. My concern is that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.
**Model:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係暴風雨喺星期六晚發生。我擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 決定下一步之前，要記住防止損失擴大嘅緊急工程呢個詞。喺呢類事情，保障視乎保單條款、上限、不保事項同適用自付額。
**Model:** What we need to look at is make-safe work. In this type of matter, cover depends on the policy wording, limits, exclusions and any applicable excess. I’ll check the details on your case now.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the next scheduled appointment or payment arrives and this is still unresolved, what should I expect?
**Model:** 去到下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期件事仲未解決，我應該預咩情況？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，提供現有證據，並要求保險公司覆核索償。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。
**Model:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, provide the available evidence and ask the insurer to review the claim. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Will photos, repair quote and claim number be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?
**Model:** 我有相片、維修報價同索償編號。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你而家開唔開到嗰啲紀錄？
**Model:** Can you access those records right now?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve got nearly everything, but one item isn’t with me.
**Model:** 差唔多全部有，不過仲有一樣冇帶喺身。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 先經保險公司索償網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。
**Model:** Start with the requested material through the insurer claims portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** How can I tell they’ve actually received the documents, I don’t want to find out later that the files never arrived.
**Model:** 我點樣先知佢哋真係收到啲文件，我唔想遲啲先發現原來啲文件根本冇收到。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 保存確認編號。預大約三至四星期；期間，採取合理措施防止損失擴大，並保留證據。
**Model:** Save the online confirmation. Allow about three to four weeks; during that time, take reasonable steps to prevent further loss and keep evidence.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the due time passes without a response, do I call about the current application, or start again?
**Model:** 如果應該有回覆嗰陣都冇聲氣，我係咪應該問返而家嗰份申請，定係重新開始？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，先用內部爭議處理程序，如投訴合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。
**Model:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, use internal dispute resolution, then AFCA if the complaint is eligible.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember make-safe work. That should help me explain the issue clearly.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住防止損失擴大嘅緊急工程。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。

## D089 — Travel insurance claim

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** You asked us to explain a travel-insurance claim. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
**Model:** 你之前要求我哋解釋旅遊保險索償。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係。我見到額外住宿費係六百八十澳元，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
**Model:** Yes. I can see that extra accommodation cost $680, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The key expression is covered event. The important general point is that cover depends on the policy wording, limits, exclusions and any applicable excess.
**Model:** 我哋而家要睇受保事件。一般最重要嘅一點係保障視乎保單條款、上限、不保事項同適用自付額。我即刻幫你對一對件紀錄。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我需要喺下星期五之前處理。否則，我擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。
**Model:** I need to deal with it before next Friday. Otherwise, I’m worried that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Just checking: that’s still the latest version you received?
**Model:** 確認一下，呢個係你最近收到嗰個版本？

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個而家仲係最新資料。
**Model:** That’s still the current information.

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The safest next step is to provide the available evidence and ask the insurer to review the claim. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.
**Model:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係提供現有證據，並要求保險公司覆核索償。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有航空公司通知、收據同行程表，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
**Model:** I have airline notice, receipts and itinerary, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Can you access the evidence we just mentioned?
**Model:** 頭先提過嗰啲證明你而家開唔開到？

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 重要嗰啲喺晒度，淨係欠一份證明。
**Model:** The important ones are here. I’m missing one supporting record.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the insurer claims portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
**Model:** 應該。經保險公司索償網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 佢哋未核對完份紀錄之前，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？
**Model:** Before they finish reviewing the record, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about five business days. Meanwhile, take reasonable steps to prevent further loss and keep evidence.
**Model:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約五個工作日。期間，採取合理措施防止損失擴大，並保留證據。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果收到書面決定之後我都仲唔滿意，我下一步應該搵邊個，先唔使又打同一輪電話？
**Model:** If I’m still unhappy after I get the written decision, who should I contact next instead of making the same phone call again?

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then use internal dispute resolution, then AFCA if the complaint is eligible.
**Model:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，先用內部爭議處理程序，如投訴合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

### Segment 16 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將受保事件同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
**Model:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of covered event with the reference number for future contact.

## D090 — Private health waiting period

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我哋睇下治療前私人醫療保險保障嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。
**Model:** Let’s check the progress of private-health cover before treatment. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Earlier I was told the procedure is booked for 4 November. I’m still concerned that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.
**Model:** 之前有人話手術預約係十一月四日。我仍然擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係等候期。要留意等候期同保障金額會視乎保單、保障級別同治療類別。
**Model:** What we need to look at is waiting period. Remember that waiting periods and benefits depend on the policy, level of cover and treatment category. I’ll check the details on your case now.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Could you check the file once more before the end of this month, just in case I’ve left something out?
**Model:** 今個月底之前可唔可以再幫我對一次份紀錄，睇吓有冇漏？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 下一步係要求保險公司書面確認計劃治療係咪適用等候期。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。
**Model:** The next step is to ask the insurer to confirm in writing whether a waiting period applies to the planned treatment. The service can then check whether anything further is required.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have policy details and hospital quote. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗保單資料同醫院報價。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。用保險公司會員網上平台提交，之後保存確認編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。
**Model:** Yes. Use the insurer’s member portal, then save the confirmation number. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** After I submit it, what’s a sensible time to ask for an update?
**Model:** 我交咗之後，大概幾時再問吓會比較合理？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約七至十個工作日。期間，保留治療預約同書面費用估算，亦唔好自行假設保險一定賠，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。
**Model:** Allow about seven to ten business days. During that period, keep the treatment booking and written cost estimate and do not assume the insurer will cover it, and check the official contact channel regularly.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Suppose one of my details changes during the assessment, what’s the safest way to update it?
**Model:** 假如評估期間我有一項資料改咗，點樣更新會最穩陣？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，先用保險公司投訴程序，如未解決再查詢合適外部投訴機構。
**Model:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the insurer complaint process and ask about the appropriate external complaints body if unresolved.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down waiting period so I can refer to the matter accurately.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低等候期，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。

## D091 — Premium renewal

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Good morning. You’re speaking with an insurance claims officer. I’m calling about an insurance renewal premium. All right, I’ve got the matter on screen. What do you need help with first?
**Model:** 早晨。呢度係保險索償主任。我聯絡你係關於保險續保保費。好，我而家睇到件事嘅資料。你想我先幫你處理邊一部分？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我最擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。我想喺通知所列日期之前處理好。
**Model:** My concern is that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid. I’d like this resolved before the date shown on the notice.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The part that matters here is insurance premium. cover depends on the policy wording, limits, exclusions and any applicable excess. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
**Model:** 我哋而家要睇保費。保障視乎保單條款、上限、不保事項同適用自付額。我即刻幫你對一對件紀錄。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我份文件寫住全年保費由一千二百四十加到一千五百二十澳元。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
**Model:** My paperwork says the annual premium rose from $1,240 to $1,520. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The record matches that detail. Next, provide the available evidence and ask the insurer to review the claim. Check every date and number before submitting.
**Model:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係提供現有證據，並要求保險公司覆核索償。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗續保通知同保單資料表。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
**Model:** I have renewal notice and policy schedule. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use the insurer claims portal. Upload renewal notice and policy schedule, then save the receipt or reference.
**Model:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用保險公司索償網上平台。上載續保通知同保單資料表，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
**Model:** How long should I wait? I’m worried that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, take reasonable steps to prevent further loss and keep evidence. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
**Model:** 預大約兩星期。期間，採取合理措施防止損失擴大，並保留證據。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果個結果我點睇都覺得唔對路，係咪有正式嘅覆核程序可以跟？
**Model:** If the result still looks wrong to me, is there a formal review process I can use?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, use internal dispute resolution, then AFCA if the complaint is eligible.
**Model:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，先用內部爭議處理程序，如投訴合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔該。我提交之前會再對一次啲資料。
**Model:** Thank you. I’ll check the details once more before I submit anything.

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That makes sense. I’ll sort out the missing item and keep the reference number.
**Model:** 咁就明喇。我會補返欠嗰份資料，再留低個參考編號。

## D092 — Contents theft claim

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝你打嚟保險索償主任。我見到有一個關於家居財物盜竊索償嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。
**Model:** Thanks for calling an insurance claims officer. I can see an enquiry about a contents-theft claim. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I received an update today. It mentions proof of ownership, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.
**Model:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到物品擁有證明。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢個詞重要，因為保障視乎保單條款、上限、不保事項同適用自付額。你目前紀錄亦顯示估計損失大約三千七百澳元。
**Model:** That term matters because cover depends on the policy wording, limits, exclusions and any applicable excess. Your current record also says the estimated loss is about $3,700.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before the next scheduled appointment or payment, because the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.
**Model:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前知道，因為索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要提供現有證據，並要求保險公司覆核索償。
**Model:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, provide the available evidence and ask the insurer to review the claim.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’ve gathered police event number, photos and bank records. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗警方事故編號、相片同銀行紀錄。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你今日可唔可以畀我睇吓啲證明資料？
**Model:** Can you show me the supporting records today?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I brought most of it, but one page is at home.
**Model:** 大部分帶咗，不過有一頁留咗喺屋企。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用保險公司索償網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。
**Model:** Use the insurer claims portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If I send everything today, what should I do while they assess it? I don’t want to create another problem.
**Model:** 如果我今日交齊啲資料，佢哋評估嗰陣咁我應該點做呀？我唔想又整多個問題出嚟。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 一般參考時間係大約三至四星期。等候期間，採取合理措施防止損失擴大，並保留證據，並留意任何正式訊息。
**Model:** The usual guide is about three to four weeks. While you wait, take reasonable steps to prevent further loss and keep evidence, and monitor any official messages.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the final answer goes against me, will they tell me what part of the application caused the problem?
**Model:** 如果最後個決定對我不利，佢哋會唔會講明係申請邊一部分出問題？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，先用內部爭議處理程序，如投訴合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。
**Model:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, use internal dispute resolution, then AFCA if the complaint is eligible.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use proof of ownership when I contact the service again.
**Model:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返物品擁有證明呢個詞。

## D093 — AFCA complaint

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’m following up on an unresolved insurance complaint. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
**Model:** 我係跟進未解決嘅保險投訴。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係最終回覆信喺八月八日收到。我擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。
**Model:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that the final response letter arrived on 8 August. My concern is that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Before deciding the next step, remember the term Australian Financial Complaints Authority, or AFCA. In this type of matter, AFCA eligibility depends on the complaint type, time limits and prior handling by the financial firm.
**Model:** 決定下一步之前，要記住澳洲金融投訴局，即係 AFCA呢個詞。喺呢類事情，AFCA 資格視乎投訴類別、時限同金融機構之前嘅處理。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果到下星期五都未搞掂，會點㗎？我驚會錯過下一步。
**Model:** If this isn’t sorted out by next Friday, what happens? I’m worried I’ll miss the next step.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, prepare the final response and claim documents and check the AFCA complaint requirements. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
**Model:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，準備最終回覆同索償文件，並核實 AFCA 投訴要求。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有最終回覆、保單同索償文件。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
**Model:** Will final response, policy and claim documents be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Have you brought the records we just discussed?
**Model:** 頭先講嗰啲紀錄你有冇帶嚟？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 主要紀錄有齊，仲有一份文件要下載返。
**Model:** I have the main records. One document still needs to be downloaded.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Start with the requested material through the AFCA online complaint service. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
**Model:** 先經AFCA 網上投訴服務提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我點樣先知佢哋真係收到啲文件，我想有個編號，之後跟進都有嘢可以講。
**Model:** How can I tell they’ve actually received the documents, I want something I can refer to if I need to follow it up.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Note the lodgement reference. Allow about five business days; during that time, keep all correspondence and the financial firm’s final response.
**Model:** 留返提交收據。預大約五個工作日；期間，保留所有通訊同金融機構嘅最終回覆。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果過咗嗰個時間都冇更新，我應該跟返原本個申請，定係再交一份？
**Model:** If there’s no update after that period, should I follow up the existing request or submit another one?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, follow the complaint steps and information requested through the AFCA process.
**Model:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，按 AFCA 程序所要求嘅投訴步驟同資料處理。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住澳洲金融投訴局，即係 AFCA。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
**Model:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Australian Financial Complaints Authority, or AFCA. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

## D094 — JobSeeker reporting

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 你之前要求我哋解釋JobSeeker 收入申報。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。
**Model:** You asked us to explain JobSeeker income reporting. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. I can see that earnings were $620 before tax this reporting period, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.
**Model:** 係。我見到今個申報期稅前收入六百二十澳元，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢件事最緊要先搞清楚JobSeeker 求職者補助金。一般最重要嘅一點係資格同付款金額會視乎你嘅情況同紀錄資料。我幫你睇吓你個情況實際點樣套用。
**Model:** The key expression is JobSeeker Payment. The important general point is that eligibility and payment rates depend on your circumstances and the information on record.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I need to deal with it before the end of this month. Otherwise, I’m worried that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.
**Model:** 我需要喺今個月底之前處理。否則，我擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我確認一下，嗰個日期而家仲啱？
**Model:** Can I confirm that date is still correct?

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, that’s definitely the latest detail I have.
**Model:** 係，呢個肯定係我手上最新嗰份資料。

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。
**Model:** The safest next step is to update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have pay slip and reporting details, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?
**Model:** 我有糧單同申報資料，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？
**Model:** Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Nearly all. I can get the missing one this evening.
**Model:** 差唔多齊，我今晚可以攞埋欠嗰份。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該。經你已連結嘅 myGov 服務上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Yes. Upload the evidence through your linked myGov service and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** During the assessment, could anything else happen that I need to prepare for?
**Model:** 評估期間，仲有冇其他情況我應該早啲有準備？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約七至十個工作日。期間，按要求申報變更，並保留已提交資料嘅證明。
**Model:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about seven to ten business days. Meanwhile, report required changes and keep evidence of what you submit.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the reasons don’t match what actually happened, what’s the proper next step? I don’t want to keep ringing different people.
**Model:** 如果啲理由同實際發生嘅事對唔上，正確下一步係咩？我唔想不停打去唔同地方。

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。
**Model:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.

### Segment 16 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of JobSeeker Payment with the reference number for future contact.
**Model:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將JobSeeker 求職者補助金同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。

## D095 — Rent Assistance update

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Let’s check the progress of updated rent details. I can see the notes from your enquiry. What’s the part you’re most concerned about?
**Model:** 我哋睇下更新租金資料嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我睇到你之前個查詢紀錄。你而家最擔心係邊一部分呀？

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 之前有人話週租由四百七十加到五百一十澳元。我仍然擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。
**Model:** Earlier I was told rent increased from $470 to $510 a week. I’m still concerned that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** For this matter, we need to be clear about Rent Assistance. Remember that eligibility and payment rates depend on your circumstances and the information on record. Let me check how that applies to you.
**Model:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係租金援助。要留意資格同付款金額會視乎你嘅情況同紀錄資料。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 去到通知所列日期之前，你可唔可以幫我睇吓仲欠唔欠資料？
**Model:** Before the date shown on the notice, can you check whether you still need anything from me?

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The next step is to update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
**Model:** 下一步係更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗新租約或者加租通知。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
**Model:** I have new lease or rent-increase notice. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Use your linked myGov service, then save the online confirmation. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
**Model:** 可以。用你已連結嘅 myGov 服務提交，之後記低提交參考編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 啲文件交齊之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？
**Model:** Once the documents are in, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Allow about two weeks. During that period, report required changes and keep evidence of what you submit, and check the official contact channel regularly.
**Model:** 預大約兩星期。期間，按要求申報變更，並保留已提交資料嘅證明，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果佢哋未處理完之前我搬屋或者換電話，我係咪更新返而家個申請，定係等有決定先？
**Model:** If I change address or phone number before they finish, do I update the current request or wait for the decision first?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.
**Model:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低租金援助，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
**Model:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down Rent Assistance so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D096 — Child Care Subsidy hours

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 早晨。呢度係Services Australia 或社區支援職員。我聯絡你係關於托兒使用情況變更。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？
**Model:** Good morning. You’re speaking with a Services Australia or community support officer. I’m calling about changed childcare use. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My concern is that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly. I’d like this resolved before the next scheduled appointment or payment.
**Model:** 我最擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。我想喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理好。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 呢件事最緊要先搞清楚托兒津貼，即係 CCS。資格同付款金額會視乎你嘅情況同紀錄資料。我幫你睇吓你個情況實際點樣套用。
**Model:** What we need to look at is Child Care Subsidy, or CCS. eligibility and payment rates depend on your circumstances and the information on record. I’ll check the details on your case now.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** My paperwork says the child now attends care four days a week. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?
**Model:** 我份文件寫住小朋友而家每星期返四日托兒。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。
**Model:** The record matches that detail. Next, update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence. Check every date and number before submitting.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have childcare enrolment and activity details. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗托兒註冊同活動資料。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 除非通知另有指示，否則用你已連結嘅 myGov 服務。上載托兒註冊同活動資料，之後保存收據或者參考編號。
**Model:** Unless your notice says otherwise, use your linked myGov service. Upload childcare enrolment and activity details, then save the receipt or reference.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** How long should I wait? I’m worried that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?
**Model:** 通常要等幾耐？我擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約三至四星期。期間，按要求申報變更，並保留已提交資料嘅證明。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。
**Model:** Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, report required changes and keep evidence of what you submit. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If the result still looks wrong to me, is there a formal review process I can use?
**Model:** 如果個結果我點睇都覺得唔對路，係咪有正式嘅覆核程序可以跟？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。
**Model:** Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks for your help. I’ll follow those steps and check the next notice carefully.
**Model:** 唔該晒。我會照住啲步驟做，之後收到新通知亦會睇清楚。

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔該。我會保存收據，同埋留返啲文件副本。
**Model:** Thanks. I’ll make sure I keep the receipt and a copy of the documents.

## D097 — Parenting Payment enquiry

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks for calling a Services Australia or community support officer. I can see an enquiry about a Parenting Payment enquiry. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
**Model:** 多謝你打嚟Services Australia 或社區支援職員。我見到有一個關於Parenting Payment 查詢嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我今日收到更新，入面提到育兒補助金。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
**Model:** I received an update today. It mentions Parenting Payment, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That term matters because eligibility and payment rates depend on your circumstances and the information on record. Your current record also says the separation date was 2 August.
**Model:** 呢個詞重要，因為資格同付款金額會視乎你嘅情況同紀錄資料。你目前紀錄亦顯示分居日期係八月二日。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下星期五之前知道，因為款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。
**Model:** So that detail still applies? I need to know before next Friday, because my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence.
**Model:** 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我已經準備咗身份、收入同家庭資料。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
**Model:** I’ve gathered identity, income and household details. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Can you access those records right now?
**Model:** 你而家開唔開到嗰啲紀錄？

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 大部分有呀，不過仲有一份喺屋企。
**Model:** I have most of them; one is still at home.

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Use your linked myGov service unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
**Model:** 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用你已連結嘅 myGov 服務。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我正式交咗資料之後，等緊期間有冇啲嘢一定要做或者唔好做呀？
**Model:** After I lodge the information, is there anything I should or shouldn’t do while I’m waiting?

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The usual guide is about five business days. While you wait, report required changes and keep evidence of what you submit, and monitor any official messages.
**Model:** 一般參考時間係大約五個工作日。等候期間，按要求申報變更，並保留已提交資料嘅證明，並留意任何正式訊息。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我收到一個負面決定，我可唔可以先攞書面理由，再諗下一步？
**Model:** If I get a negative decision, can I get the reasons in writing before I decide what to do next?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.
**Model:** 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返育兒補助金呢個詞。
**Model:** All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use Parenting Payment when I contact the service again.

## D098 — Carer Payment enquiry

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我係跟進照顧者補助查詢。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。
**Model:** I’m following up on a carer-payment enquiry. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Thanks. The detail I was given is that work has reduced to twelve hours a week. My concern is that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.
**Model:** 多謝。我收到嘅資料係工作減到每星期十二個鐘。我擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 決定下一步之前，要記住照顧者補助金呢個詞。喺呢類事情，資格同付款金額會視乎你嘅情況同紀錄資料。
**Model:** Before deciding the next step, remember the term Carer Payment. In this type of matter, eligibility and payment rates depend on your circumstances and the information on record.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** What are the consequences if I can’t resolve this by the end of this month?
**Model:** 如果我喺今個月底之前處理唔到，實際會有咩後果？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 唔好自行假設結果。首先，更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。
**Model:** Don’t assume the outcome. First, update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Will care details, income records and medical forms be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?
**Model:** 我有照顧資料、收入紀錄同醫療表格。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？
**Model:** Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have the main ones. One file is on my home computer.
**Model:** 主要嗰幾份有，一個檔案仲喺屋企部電腦。

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 先經你已連結嘅 myGov 服務提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。
**Model:** Start with the requested material through your linked myGov service. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Once I lodge it, how will I know it’s on their system, That date could matter if there’s a dispute later.
**Model:** 我正式提交之後，點知系統已經有紀錄，如果之後有爭議，嗰個日期可能好重要㗎。

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 保存網上確認資料。預大約七至十個工作日；期間，按要求申報變更，並保留已提交資料嘅證明。
**Model:** Note the lodgement reference. Allow about seven to ten business days; during that time, report required changes and keep evidence of what you submit.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** If there’s no update after that period, should I follow up the existing request or submit another one?
**Model:** 如果過咗嗰個時間都冇更新，我應該跟返原本個申請，定係再交一份？

### Segment 13 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。
**Model:** Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.

### Segment 14 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Carer Payment. That should help me explain the issue clearly.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住照顧者補助金。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。

### Segment 15 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 好。我攞到欠嗰份文件就補返，到時再跟住處理。
**Model:** Okay. I’ll bring the missing document when I have it and we can go from there.

## D099 — NDIS plan manager

### Segment 1 — EN → YUE
**Source:** You asked us to explain an NDIS plan-management question. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
**Model:** 你之前要求我哋解釋NDIS 計劃管理問題。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

### Segment 2 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係。我見到治療發票係二百四十五澳元，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
**Model:** Yes. I can see that the therapy invoice is $245, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

### Segment 3 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The key expression is plan manager. The important general point is that invoice handling depends on the participant’s plan-management arrangement, funding and service agreement.
**Model:** 我哋而家要睇計劃管理人。一般最重要嘅一點係發票處理會視乎參加者嘅計劃管理安排、資金同服務協議。我即刻幫你對一對件紀錄。

### Segment 4 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我需要喺通知所列日期之前處理。否則，我擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。
**Model:** I need to deal with it before the date shown on the notice. Otherwise, I’m worried that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.

### Segment 5 — EN → YUE
**Source:** One last check on that point: has it changed at all?
**Model:** 呢一點我最後確認多次：之後有冇改過？

### Segment 6 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 係，最新訊息都係咁講。
**Model:** Yes, the latest message says the same thing.

### Segment 7 — EN → YUE
**Source:** The safest next step is to check the service agreement and send the invoice through the correct plan-management process. Keep the wording factual and include the relevant dates and amounts.
**Model:** 最穩妥嘅下一步係核對服務協議，並用正確計劃管理程序提交發票。內容要按事實寫，並包括相關日期同金額。

### Segment 8 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我有NDIS 計劃、服務協議同發票，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
**Model:** I have NDIS plan, service agreement and invoice, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

### Segment 9 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Do you have the supporting documents with you today?
**Model:** 你今日有冇帶埋啲證明文件？

### Segment 10 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 大部分都喺度，最後嗰份我今晚可以補交。
**Model:** Most are here; I can send the last one tonight.

### Segment 11 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Yes. Upload the evidence through the plan manager’s invoice portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
**Model:** 應該。經計劃管理人嘅發票網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

### Segment 12 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 佢哋未核對完份紀錄之前，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？
**Model:** Before they finish reviewing the record, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?

### Segment 13 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, keep the invoice and service-delivery evidence while payment is being checked.
**Model:** 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約兩星期。期間，付款核對期間保留發票同服務提供證明。

### Segment 14 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 如果我睇完書面決定都仲係唔明，我要點樣跟正式程序提出覆核？
**Model:** If the written decision still doesn’t make sense, how do I challenge it through the right process?

### Segment 15 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask the plan manager to explain the invoice decision and the next available dispute step.
**Model:** 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，要求計劃管理人解釋發票決定同下一個可用爭議處理步驟。

### Segment 16 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 多謝。我會照做，亦會將計劃管理人同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
**Model:** Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of plan manager with the reference number for future contact.

## D100 — Emergency relief referral

### Segment 1 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我哋睇下緊急援助要求嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。
**Model:** Let’s check the progress of an emergency-relief request. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.

### Segment 2 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Earlier I was told the next income payment is due in five days. I’m still concerned that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.
**Model:** 之前有人話下一筆收入五日後先收到。我仍然擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。

### Segment 3 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係緊急生活援助。要留意資格同付款金額會視乎你嘅情況同紀錄資料。
**Model:** For this matter, we need to be clear about emergency relief. Remember that eligibility and payment rates depend on your circumstances and the information on record. Let me check how that applies to you.

### Segment 4 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I’d rather fix any missing information now. Is there anything else you need before the next scheduled appointment or payment?
**Model:** 如果有資料欠咗我想而家補。下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前仲有冇嘢要交？

### Segment 5 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 下一步係更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。
**Model:** The next step is to update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence. The service can then check whether anything further is required.

### Segment 6 — EN → YUE
**Source:** I have photo ID and current financial information. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?
**Model:** 我已經準備咗有相身份證同現時財務資料。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？

### Segment 7 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 可以。用你已連結嘅 myGov 服務提交，之後保存確認編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。
**Model:** Yes. Use your linked myGov service, then keep the submission receipt. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.

### Segment 8 — EN → YUE
**Source:** After the upload is complete, what’s a sensible time to ask for an update?
**Model:** 上載完成之後，大概幾時再問吓會比較合理？

### Segment 9 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 預大約三至四星期。期間，按要求申報變更，並保留已提交資料嘅證明，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。
**Model:** Allow about three to four weeks. During that period, report required changes and keep evidence of what you submit, and check the official contact channel regularly.

### Segment 10 — EN → YUE
**Source:** Suppose one of my details changes during the assessment, should I tell them straight away?
**Model:** 假如評估期間我有一項資料改咗，我係咪應該即刻通知佢哋？

### Segment 11 — YUE → EN
**Source:** 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。
**Model:** Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.

### Segment 12 — EN → YUE
**Source:** That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down emergency relief so I can refer to the matter accurately.
**Model:** 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低緊急生活援助，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
